To address this, we propose {\bf A}daptive {\bf C}omplementary {\bf E}nhancement, a plug-and-play, model-agnostic strategy that reintegrates the information discarded in coarsening: ACE learns a projector for re-constructing original node features and applies \textit{anisotropic structural regularization} to embed local heterophily. We further adopt \textit{homoscedastic uncertainty weighting} to adaptively balance the combined training objective of primary coarsened-graph training loss and full-graph auxiliary loss with augmented node features re-constructed by the heterophily-aware projector. Extensive experiments show that ACE drives consistent gains on heterophilic benchmarks while preserving competitive results on homophilic graphs with minimal computational overhead. Code is available at the GitHub repository: https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/ACE.

</details>


### [89] [MissHyper: Restoring Clinical Synchronicity in Missingness-Guided Hypergraph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21922)
*Mingyi Ma,Qingxiong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对临床不规则多元时间序列中事件共时间戳结构被过早扁平化的问题，提出MissHyper模型，通过缺失感知的预传播同步性恢复机制，在消息传递前恢复同一时间戳记录的局部上下文，显著提升多步预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件中心模型过早展平共时间戳结构，将同一时刻获取的测量值作为孤立节点嵌入，导致局部患者状态上下文在后续消息传递层之前不可用，形成预传播表示瓶颈。

Method: 提出MissHyper模型：1）为每个事件增加局部支撑密度线索；2）聚合同一时间戳记录以恢复患者状态上下文；3）利用缺失引导的门控自适应融合节点特定证据与恢复的上下文。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV三个数据集上，MissHyper在多步预测中取得一致提升，且优于强超图基线模型。

Conclusion: 改善事件初始化能够有效提升稀疏临床预测性能，无需重新设计下游传播架构；快照恢复、自适应融合和支撑密度编码均有贡献，事件初始化是稀疏临床预测的关键设计维度。

Abstract: Clinical irregular multivariate time series are shaped not only by physiological dynamics but also by the measurement process that determines when and what to observe. In event-centric models, however, co-timestamp structure can be flattened too early: measurements acquired at the same timestamp are embedded as isolated nodes, leaving local patient-state context unavailable until later message-passing layers. We study this pre-propagation representation bottleneck and address it by restoring co-timestamp context before message passing begins. We propose MissHyper, a missingness-guided hypergraph forecasting model with pre-propagation synchronicity restoration. MissHyper augments each event with a local support-density cue, aggregates co-timestamp records to recover patient-state context, and uses a missingness-guided gate to adaptively fuse node-specific evidence with the recovered context. Across PhysioNet 2012, MIMIC-III, and MIMIC-IV, MissHyper achieves consistent gains in multi-step forecasting and outperforms a strong hypergraph baseline. These results suggest that improving event initialization can benefit sparse clinical forecasting without requiring a redesigned downstream propagation architecture. Ablations indicate that snapshot restoration, adaptive fusion, and support-density encoding all contribute, pointing to event initialization as a critical design axis for sparse clinical forecasting.

</details>


### [90] [RIS-Kernel: A Model-Agnostic Architecture for Long-Context LLM Inference via Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21927)
*Anderson R. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIS是一种模型无关的推理引擎，通过稀疏随机几何将自注意力复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N log N)，在商品级硬件上实现长上下文LLM推理。在32K token下，1%密度+70个种子的RIS-Stochastic达到75%准确率，超越原生密集注意力的71.88%；在65K token下，密集注意力因OOM失败，RIS仍能获得显著检索增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的全自注意力机制具有O(N²)的计算复杂度，限制了长上下文文档分析至65,536个token，并且需要昂贵的GPU集群。作者旨在解决这一可扩展性瓶颈，使得长上下文LLM推理能够在普通学术硬件上运行。

Method: 提出Reduced Interaction Sampling (RIS)推理引擎，这是一种模型无关的架构。在不修改权重的情况下，利用稀疏随机几何将自注意力复杂度降至O(N log N)，使其适配商品级内存限制。包含两种变体：RIS-Stochastic（随机采样）和RIS-Structural（结构采样）。

Result: 在Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct上验证：(1) 32K token时，RIS-Stochastic在1%密度+70种子下达75.00%准确率（超密集基线71.88%），5%密度+10种子持平基线；(2) 65K token时，密集注意力OOM，RIS检索增益达14.06个百分点（相对零上下文基线51.56%），McNemar检验p=0.078边际显著。所有实验在16-128GB RAM的未加速CPU服务器上完成。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力可作为正则化器：低密度多种子过滤序列级噪声，高密度则重新引入干扰噪声。RIS证明了在无需GPU加速的商品级硬件上实现长上下文LLM推理的可行性，为长文档分析提供了经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Full self-attention in large language models scales as O(N^2), which limits long-context document analysis to 65,536 tokens and requires costly GPU clusters. The Reduced Interaction Sampling (RIS) inference engine addresses this constraint as a model-agnostic architecture. Without modifying weights, RIS reduces self-attention complexity to O(N log N) using sparse stochastic geometry that fits within commodity memory limits. We validate RIS on Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct across two regimes. In controlled evaluations at 32,768 tokens (where native dense attention serves as the upper bound), RIS-Stochastic at 1% density and 70 ensemble seeds achieves 75.00% accuracy, outperforming the native dense baseline (71.88%), while RIS-Stochastic at 5% density and 10 seeds matches it (71.88%). This demonstrates that sparse attention acts as a regularizer: low density (1%) over multiple seeds filters out sequence-level noise, whereas higher density (5%) reintroduces distractor noise. Under the tightest budget, RIS-Structural reaches 68.75% accuracy at 1% density with just 10 seeds, recovering 75% of the contextual gap relative to the zero-context floor (59.38%). At 65,536 tokens, where dense attention triggers out-of-memory faults, RIS yields retrieval gains of up to 14.06 percentage points over the zero-context floor (51.56%), which is confirmed as marginally significant under McNemar's paired test (p = 0.078 < 0.10). All evaluations run on commodity, unaccelerated CPU servers (16-128 GB of RAM), demonstrating that long-context LLM inference is feasible on standard academic hardware without GPU acceleration.

</details>


### [91] [LatentFlow: Visual Analytics for Latent Space Analysis in Molecular Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21941)
*Shiyi Liu,Jiaqing Chen,Nicholas Hadler,Rostyslav Hnatyshyn,Michael W. Mahoney,Talita Perciano,John F. Hartwig,Gunther H. Weber,Ross Maciejewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentFlow，一个用于分析分子图神经网络潜在空间的可视分析系统。该系统通过改进的Sankey图追踪聚类在层间和模型状态间的变化，将潜在空间聚类与代表性分子及共享子结构关联，并支持引入领域知识进行比较。案例研究表明，LatentFlow帮助科学家理解潜在空间演化、识别有意义的分子模式并解释模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在分析神经网络潜在空间时，无法有效支持跨层和跨模型状态（如训练轮次、模型配置、输入数据）的比较，导致难以理解潜在空间如何随模型演化或与化学概念相关联。

Method: LatentFlow将分子嵌入聚类，使用改进的Sankey图追踪聚类在层间和模型状态间的变化，将聚类与代表性分子及其共享子结构关联，并允许科学家引入领域知识与潜在空间模式进行比较。

Result: 通过两个案例研究评估，结果表明LatentFlow帮助科学家理解潜在空间演化，识别有意义的分子模式，并更好地解释模型行为。

Conclusion: LatentFlow有效支持了分子GNN潜在空间的分析，能够揭示潜在空间演化规律和化学上有意义的分子模式，提升模型可解释性。

Abstract: Chemists and materials scientists increasingly use machine learning models, such as graph neural networks (GNNs), to predict properties of molecules and the outcomes of their reactions. Beyond predictive performance, understanding how these models organize chemical information internally in their latent spaces, i.e., the embeddings of the molecules, is critical. Analyzing latent spaces helps diagnose model behavior and assess whether the learned embeddings are organized in ways that reflect meaningful chemical relationships. Unfortunately, existing methods provide limited support for analyzing latent spaces across layers and across different model states (e.g., training epochs, model configurations, and input data), making it difficult to understand how these latent spaces evolve throughout a model or relate to chemical concepts. We present LatentFlow, a visual analytics system developed in collaboration with a domain expert for analyzing latent spaces in molecular GNNs. LatentFlow groups embeddings into clusters and supports exploration of latent spaces by tracking how these clusters change across layers and model states using a modified Sankey diagram. To support interpretation, LatentFlow links these clusters to representative molecules and their shared substructures, and it allows scientists to introduce their own domain knowledge and compare it with the patterns found in the latent spaces. We evaluate LatentFlow through two case studies. The results show that LatentFlow helps scientists understand how latent spaces evolve, identify meaningful molecular patterns, and better interpret model behavior.

</details>


### [92] [Multi-Agent Debate and Visual Information Extraction for SeePhys Pro: A 1st-Place Technical Report from ICML 2026 AI4Math Track 3 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21946)
*Jiseok Kwak,Suhyeon Jo,Taewoo Kim,Yeongmin Kim,Byeonghu Na,Il-chul Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段框架来解决视觉物理问题：首先通过视觉信息提取将图像中的内容转化为文本，然后通过多智能体辩论协调三个求解器进行推理。该框架在SeePhys Pro挑战赛中获得第一名，准确率从0.643提升至0.802（公开集）。研究发现：多智能体协调的价值在于可靠的答案选择而非额外的辩论过程，且图像辅助的价值与问题信息在图像中的占比正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉物理问题中，当关键信息存在于图像而非文本时，大语言模型的表现大幅下降，且这一模态差距随着问题信息向图像迁移而扩大。现有方法缺乏有效弥合这一差距的框架。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 视觉信息提取阶段：将图像内容重新表达为求解器可读的文本，缩小模态差距；2) 推理阶段：通过多智能体辩论协调三个异构求解器进行答案推理。

Result: 公开集上准确率从单智能体基线的0.643提升至0.802，私有集整体准确率0.743，在公开和私有排行榜均获得第一名。

Conclusion: 多智能体协调的增益主要来自可靠的答案选择而非辩论过程本身；图像辅助的价值与问题信息被锁定在图像中的比例正相关。

Abstract: This technical report presents our approach to Challenge Track~3: SeePhys Pro at the 3rd AI for Math Workshop, where the task is to answer college-level physics questions whose statement and figure may be given partly or entirely as an image. Visual physics problems become substantially harder for large language models when the decisive information resides in a figure rather than in the text, and this modality gap widens as more of the problem migrates into the image. We address the task with a two-stage framework: a visual information extraction stage that re-expresses figure content as solver-readable text to close the modality gap, and a reasoning stage that orchestrates three heterogeneous solvers through multi-agent debate. Our analysis yields two findings: the gain from orchestration comes from reliable answer selection rather than from additional debate, and the value of a figure aid scales with how much of the problem is locked inside the image. The resulting pipeline improves overall accuracy over a single-agent baseline from 0.643 to 0.802 on the public split, and won 1st place on both the public and the private leaderboard (private overall 0.743).

</details>


### [93] [MA-DAR: Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing for Continual Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21949)
*Xiangjun Shi,Chong Mu,Jinchuan Zhang,Lizong Zhang,Yuefeng He,Shang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对持续时序知识图谱推理中回放表示与当前表示融合时的表示冲突问题（包括范数支配和语义模糊），提出了一种轻量级即插即用框架MA-DAR，通过流形对齐、动态自适应路由和极化正则化机制来缓解冲突，提升持续推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于回放的持续学习方法主要关注"回放什么"，而忽视了"回放表示如何与当前表示融合"这一关键问题。直接融合会导致范数支配和语义模糊两种表示冲突，从而降低持续推理性能。

Method: 提出MA-DAR框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 流形对齐：将回放表示与当前表示对齐到共享流形以缓解分布差异；2) 动态门控机制：学习维度级别的融合权重，自适应确定回放表示和当前表示的贡献；3) 极化正则化：通过惩罚模糊的门控决策来鼓励更确定的路由行为，实现更稳定有效的知识融合。

Result: 在四个公开持续TKG基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MA-DAR能够持续提升代表性TKG编码器的性能，并在不同回放设置下均保持有效性。消融实验和可视化分析进一步验证了流形对齐和动态自适应路由在缓解表示冲突方面的有效性。

Conclusion: MA-DAR作为一种轻量级即插即用框架，通过解决回放融合过程中的表示冲突问题，有效提升了持续时序知识图谱推理的性能。

Abstract: Continual temporal knowledge graph (TKG) reasoning aims to continuously incorporate newly emerging facts while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Replay-based continual learning has achieved promising performance by revisiting historical representations. However, existing methods primarily focus on what to replay, while largely overlooking how replayed representations should be integrated with current ones. Such direct integration often gives rise to two critical forms of representation conflict: \textit{norm domination} and \textit{semantic blurring}, ultimately degrading continual reasoning performance. To address these challenges, we propose MA-DAR (Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing), a lightweight plug-and-play framework for replay representation fusion. MA-DAR first aligns replayed and current representations onto a shared manifold to alleviate distribution discrepancies. It then employs a dynamic gating mechanism to learn dimension-wise fusion weights, adaptively determining the contribution of replayed and current representations to the fused representation. Furthermore, a polarization regularizer encourages more decisive routing behaviors by discouraging ambiguous gating decisions, resulting in more stable and effective knowledge integration. Extensive experiments on four public continual TKG benchmarks demonstrate that MA-DAR consistently improves the performance of representative TKG encoders while remaining effective under different replay settings. Comprehensive ablation studies and visualization analyses further verify the effectiveness of manifold alignment and dynamic adaptive routing in mitigating representation conflicts and improving continual reasoning.

</details>


### [94] [Teaching LLMs to Self-Evolve: Cultivating Core Meta-Skills with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21971)
*Shujin Wu,Cheng Qian,Xiusi Chen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaEvolve框架，通过数据合成管道、进化感知强化学习和推理时进化搜索，显式培养自我进化元技能（如自我反思与环境反馈利用）。在编程领域基于程序执行信号训练，在7个编码基准上超越最强基线10.01%-24.12%，在开放算法优化问题上实现46.9%相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法（如AlphaEvolve）通过环境反馈的迭代自我进化取得了显著成效，但其成功依赖的元技能（如利用环境反馈进行自我反思）在传统后训练中常被忽视。作者旨在弥补这一空白，显式培养这些元技能以提升AI的自主进化能力。

Method: MetaEvolve框架包含三部分：(1) 数据合成管道，基于程序执行的自然奖励信号（超越二元正确性）生成进化轨迹数据，每条包含当前程序、适应度分数（正确性+效率）及历史尝试；(2) 进化感知强化学习，利用测试用例执行产生的可验证奖励训练模型；(3) 推理时进化搜索。整个训练基于大规模代码数据，旨在激发领域无关的通用元技能。

Result: 在7个编码基准上，MetaEvolve在分布内任务上超越最强基线10.01%绝对性能提升，在分布外任务上提升24.12%。在完全超出训练领域的开放算法优化问题上，实现46.9%相对改进。

Conclusion: 显式培养自我进化元技能为构建更强大、能自主进化的AI提供了一条有原则的路径。元技能（而非特定任务能力）的训练可以实现跨领域迁移，解决开放性问题。

Abstract: Test-time scaling through iterative self-evolution with environment feedback, as demonstrated by AlphaEvolve, shows remarkable performance gains. We hypothesize that the success of such evolution frameworks hinges on meta-skills, such as self-reflection with environment feedback, that enable effective multi-round refinement, yet are largely neglected by traditional post-training. To bridge this gap, we present MetaEvolve, a framework designed to develop these meta-skills via a data synthesis pipeline, evolution-aware reinforcement learning (RL), and inference-time evolutionary search. Concretely, we ground MetaEvolve in coding, where program execution provides natural, continuous reward signals beyond binary correctness. Building on these signals, we synthesize evolution trajectories as training data, each containing a current program, its fitness score (combining correctness and efficiency), and a history of prior attempts, and train the model via RL with verifiable rewards derived from test case execution. By training on large-scale code data, we aim to inspire generalizable domain-agnostic meta-skills that can transfer broadly to open-ended problems where such rich training signals are scarce. Across seven coding benchmarks, MetaEvolve outperforms the strongest baseline by 10.01% absolute on in-distribution tasks and 24.12% on out-of-distribution tasks. On open-ended algorithm optimization problems entirely outside the training domain, it further achieves a 46.9% relative improvement. These results demonstrate that explicitly cultivating self-evolution meta-skills offers a principled path toward more capable and autonomously self-evolving AI.

</details>


### [95] [From Perturbation Correction to Geometry-Aware Sampling: Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling for Balanced Flat Minima in Long-Tailed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21999)
*Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling (SGS)，一种动态调整采样分布以平衡类别频率和损失景观几何特性的方法，通过频率-锐度反馈机制引导训练趋向更平坦的损失景观，在不增加额外计算开销的情况下显著提升长尾学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾学习中存在双重泛化问题：头部类别主导训练曝光，而尾部类别收敛到损失景观的更尖锐区域。传统重采样方法仅解决曝光不平衡，忽略了几何特性；现有长尾锐度感知最小化(SAM)方法仅在已有偏置小批量上修改损失或扰动。需要一种能同时调控数据暴露和优化几何的采样方法。

Method: SGS将采样分布视为优化几何的主动控制变量，动态调整后续小批量：增加低频采样类别的采样概率，同时抑制具有较大SAM诱导损失变化的类别。仅使用累积类别计数和从标准SAM更新中获得的EMA锐度估计，无需类别级扰动或额外反向传播。通过连续时间随机微分方程和采样相关PAC-Bayes分析刻画该采样过程。

Result: 在CIFAR-100 LT（不平衡比100）上，SGS-SAM相比Focal-SAM尾部准确率提升10.85个百分点，总体提升3.56个百分点。在ImageNet-LT上，相比ImbSAM尾部类别提升6.59个百分点，总体提升1.20个百分点。训练时间仅为vanilla SAM的1.02倍。

Conclusion: SGS建立了一条通过采样侧控制损失景观的路径，表明未来长尾学习方法可以联合调控数据暴露和优化几何，而非将其中任一视为固定因素。

Abstract: Long-tailed learning couples two sources of poor generalization: head classes dominate training exposure, while under-represented classes often converge to sharper regions of the loss landscape. Conventional re-sampling addresses the former without considering geometry, whereas existing long-tailed sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) methods modify losses or perturbations only after biased mini-batches have been drawn. We introduce Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling (SGS), which treats the sampling distribution as an active control variable for optimization geometry. SGS dynamically adjusts subsequent mini-batches by increasing the sampling probability of less frequently sampled classes while suppressing classes with large SAM-induced loss changes, using only cumulative class counts and EMA sharpness estimates obtained from the standard SAM update, without class-wise perturbations or additional backward passes. We characterize this sampling process through a continuous-time stochastic differential equation and a sampling-dependent PAC-Bayes analysis, explaining how frequency-sharpness feedback can move training toward a more balanced flatness profile. On CIFAR-100 LT with an imbalance ratio of 100, SGS-SAM improves Focal-SAM by 10.85 points in tail accuracy and 3.56 points overall. On ImageNet-LT, it improves ImbSAM by 6.59 points on tail classes and 1.20 points overall. Its training time is only $1.02\times$ that of vanilla SAM. Beyond these gains, SGS establishes a sampling-side route to loss-landscape control, suggesting that future long-tailed methods can jointly regulate data exposure and optimization geometry rather than treating either as fixed.

</details>


### [96] [Energy Manifold Natural Gradient Descent: Riemannian Optimization for Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22004)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMNGD（流形能量自然梯度下降），将参数约束在黎曼流形上的神经PDE求解器优化框架。通过限制能量二次模型到可行切方向并使用retraction保持约束，证明其推前方向是函数空间牛顿向量的最佳可行逼近。理论保证坐标不变性、全局一阶收敛性及对不精确求解的鲁棒性，利用Woodbury恒等式和Nyström近似实现可扩展样本空间求解。实验在神经PDE基准上取得更高精度和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能量自然梯度下降（ENGD）假设参数在无约束欧几里得域中，无法处理参数位于黎曼流形上的物理信息神经网络和变分神经PDE求解器。需要一种流形优化框架来正确处理参数约束。

Method: 提出EMNGD框架：1) 将能量诱导的二次模型限制在可行切方向；2) 使用retraction操作在优化过程中保持参数流形约束；3) 在强制性条件下，证明无阻尼EMNGD方向是函数空间牛顿向量的最佳可行逼近；4) 利用Woodbury恒等式将切空间线性系统转换到样本空间；5) 采用Nyström近似实现可扩展样本空间求解并控制方向误差。

Result: 在神经PDE基准测试上，EMNGD相比现有最优基线方法取得更高精度和更快收敛速度。Woodbury恒等式保持原始EMNGD方向不变，可扩展求解器诊断量化了预条件和残差子采样之间的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: EMNGD为流形约束参数空间的神经PDE求解提供了理论上完备的优化框架，具备坐标不变性、一阶收敛保证和计算可扩展性，在多个基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Energy natural gradient descent (ENGD) aligns parameter updates with the curvature of an underlying function-space energy, but existing formulations assume an unconstrained Euclidean parameter domain. We introduce \EMNGDfull{}, a manifold optimization framework for physics-informed and variational neural PDE solvers whose parameters lie on a Riemannian manifold. EMNGD restricts the energy-induced quadratic model to feasible tangent directions and uses retractions to preserve parameter constraints throughout optimization. Under coercivity, we prove that the push-forward of the undamped EMNGD direction is the best feasible approximation to the function-space Newton vector in the energy metric. We establish coordinate invariance, exact reduction to ENGD in Euclidean space, global first-order convergence with Armijo backtracking, and robustness to inexact tangent solves. For quadratic residual energies and generalized Gauss--Newton pullbacks, the Woodbury identity transfers the tangent system to sample space without changing the direction. Nyström approximation provides scalable sample-space solves with controlled direction error and recovers the exact direction after iterative convergence. On the evaluated neural PDE benchmarks, EMNGD achieves higher accuracy and faster convergence than the compared state-of-the-art baselines. Woodbury preserves the EMNGD direction, while scalable-solver diagnostics quantify the accuracy--cost trade-off of preconditioning and residual subsampling.

</details>


### [97] [Cross-Domain Off-Policy Evaluation and Learning for Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22012)
*Yuta Natsubori,Masataka Ushiku,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了跨域离线策略评估与学习（Cross-Domain OPE/L）的新问题设定，通过利用目标域和源域的日志数据，解决现有方法在少样本、确定性日志策略和新动作等挑战性场景下失效的问题，并开发了新的估计器和策略梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPE/L方法在面对少样本数据、确定性日志策略和新动作等常见但挑战性的场景时存在方差大或探索不足的问题，无法有效评估和优化新策略。

Method: 提出Cross-Domain OPE/L框架，同时利用目标域（待实施新策略的领域）和源域（其他相关领域）的历史日志数据，开发了新的估计器和策略梯度方法。

Result: 经验评估表明，所提方法在之前无法解决的场景中显著提升了OPE/L的性能。

Conclusion: 跨域OPE/L框架通过整合多领域数据，有效解决了传统方法在少样本、确定性策略和新动作等困境下的局限性，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Off-Policy Evaluation and Learning (OPE/L) in contextual bandits is rapidly gaining popularity in real systems because new policies can be evaluated and learned securely using only historical logged data. However, existing methods in OPE/L cannot handle many challenging but prevalent scenarios such as few-shot data, deterministic logging policies, and new actions. In many applications, such as personalized medicine, content recommendations, education, and advertising, we need to evaluate and learn new policies in the presence of these challenges. Existing methods cannot evaluate and optimize effectively in these situations due to the notorious variance issue or limited exploration in the logged data. To enable OPE/L even under these unsolved challenges, we propose a new problem setup of Cross-Domain OPE/L, where we have access not only to the logged data from the target domain in which the new policy will be implemented but also to logged datasets collected from other domains. This novel formulation is widely applicable because we can often use historical data not only from the target hospital, country, device, or user segment but also from other hospitals, countries, devices, or segments. We develop a new estimator and policy gradient method to solve OPE/L by leveraging both target and source datasets, resulting in substantially enhanced OPE/L in the previously unsolved situations in our empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [98] [DCS: A Unified Conditional Sensitivity Framework for Cross-Modal Copyright Infringement Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22035)
*Xiafeng Man*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCS框架，通过双重分支条件敏感性统计量，在无法访问训练数据的情况下检测基础模型对受版权保护内容的记忆与侵权，并将该框架实例化于线性回归、扩散模型、语言模型和多模态模型等多种架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型可能复制受版权保护的训练内容，但仅凭输出相似度不足以判定侵权，因为相似输出也可能源于公共领域概念、通用风格惯例或普通统计泛化。需要更精确的检测方法区分真正的数据记忆与普通泛化。

Method: 提出统一的事后检测框架，将版权侵权证据视为反事实条件分布偏移。通过条件差分隐私形式化这一观点，引入双分支条件敏感性（DCS）统计量：在学习分支与遗忘分支之间测量可观测差距，利用影响函数分析将其与不可获得的反事实重训练效应关联，并通过反事实隐私预算代理、局部曲率、训练集规模和扰动步长界定可观测敏感性。为区分目标特定记忆与通用微调不稳定性，进一步定义校准检测统计量，减去正交条件下的敏感性。

Result: DCS框架成功实例化于岭正则化线性回归、条件扩散模型、自回归语言模型和多模态模型，展示了通过预测差距、图像嵌入散度、token分布或熵偏移以及跨模态表示变化等不同途径评估同一原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为版权侵权检测提供了理论基础和可操作统计量，能够区分受保护内容的特定记忆与通用统计泛化，适用于多种主流模型架构。

Abstract: Currently, most foundation models can reproduce or strongly depend on copyrighted training content, but output similarity alone is insufficient for infringement detection, because similar outputs may also arise from public-domain concepts, common stylistic conventions, or ordinary statistical generalization.
  In this paper, we develops a unified post-hoc detection framework that treats copyright infringement evidence as a counterfactual conditional distribution shift: a protected target is suspicious when the model's behavior under aligned conditions would change measurably if that target were included in, or removed from, the training process.
  We formalize this view through conditional differential privacy and introduce Dual-Branch Conditional Sensitivity (DCS), an operational statistic that measures the observable gap between two locally perturbed model states. Specifically, the proposed DCS framework creates a learning branch and an unlearning branch around the deployed model, connects their displacement to the unavailable counterfactual retraining effect through influence-function analysis, and bounds the observable sensitivity by the counterfactual privacy-budget surrogate, local curvature, training-set scale, and perturbation step size. To distinguish target-specific memorization from generic fine-tuning instability, we further define a calibrated detection statistic that subtracts the sensitivity measured under orthogonal conditions.
  The DCS framework is instantiated for ridge-regularized linear regression, conditional diffusion models, autoregressive language models, and multimodal models. These instantiations show how the same principle can be evaluated through prediction gaps, image-embedding divergence, token-distribution or entropy shifts, and cross-modal representation changes.

</details>


### [99] [CEL: Comprehensive Counterfactual Explanations Library and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22045)
*Oleksii Furman,Łukasz Lenkiewicz,Marcel Musiałek,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CEL（反事实解释库），一个统一的反事实解释库和基准测试平台，包含18个数据集和14种方法的实现/复现，通过多指标评估协议实现公平、可复现的方法比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法评估存在数据划分、预测模型和评估指标不统一的问题，导致不同方法之间难以进行客观比较。缺乏一个标准化的、可复现的基准测试框架。

Method: 构建了CEL库，整合18个不同规模和复杂度的数据集，实现或复现14种主流反事实方法，设计包含有效性、覆盖率、稀疏性、邻近性和分布合理性（含密度和异常值度量）的多维评估协议。

Result: 首次在统一可复现框架下对多个反事实解释方法进行了系统性定量比较，覆盖不同规模和属性类型的多个数据集。

Conclusion: 该基准测试旨在提高可复现性，实现公平比较，并为未来反事实解释方法的发展建立工作平台。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations are a prominent approach in explainable artificial intelligence (xAI), providing actionable guidance on what input changes would alter a model's prediction to a desired outcome. While early methods primarily focused on minimal feature changes, recent work incorporates additional properties such as sparsity, actionability and plausibility. Despite this progress, fair and systematic evaluation remains challenging. Existing studies often rely on different data splits, predictive models, and evaluation metrics, which limits objective comparison across methods. To fill this gap, we introduce CEL (Counterfactual Explanations Library), a unified library and benchmark for counterfactual explanations designed to support consistent implementation and evaluation. CEL includes 18 datasets of varying size and complexity and provides implementations or reimplementations of 14 widely used counterfactual methods. Using this standardized setup, we conduct a comprehensive quantitative comparison across a variety of methods on datasets that differ in size, number, and types of attributes. The evaluation protocol incorporates multiple complementary metrics capturing validity, coverage, sparsity, proximity, and distributional plausibility, including density- and outlier-based measures to assess the realism of generated counterfactuals. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive benchmark that systematically evaluates recent counterfactual explanation methods within a unified and reproducible framework. While prior libraries and benchmarking efforts exist in the literature, many are outdated, limited in scope, or lack consistent evaluation protocols. The proposed benchmark aims to improve reproducibility, enable fair comparison, and establish a workbench for the development of future counterfactual explanation methods.

</details>


### [100] [A Leakage-Free Stacked Ensemble Method for Multiclass Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22081)
*S. P. Sharmila,Aruna Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LFS-FRAME框架，通过无泄漏堆叠策略集成KAN函数学习与XGBoost规则学习，在多元分类任务中达到89.85%（主类）和81.74%（子类）的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分类方法存在不足：规则型模型（如XGBoost）难以捕捉平滑函数关系，神经网络易过拟合且泛化能力差。现有集成学习存在性能泄漏问题。

Method: 提出LFS-FRAME框架，采用严格留出折（out-of-fold）堆叠策略确保训练与验证数据完全隔离，防止性能泄漏；集成KAN（函数学习）和XGBoost（规则学习）作为异构基学习器，元分类器基于基学习器的概率输出学习全局函数模式与决策边界。

Result: 在多类数据集上，LFS-FRAME在主类识别中达到89.85%准确率，在子类识别中达到81.74%准确率，优于强单模型基线。

Conclusion: 无泄漏的函数与规则堆叠策略能有效提升多元分类的可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Multiclass classification is a fundamental problem across a wide range of domains. It is still challenging due to possession of high inter-class similarity, class imbalance datasets, and variability in data distributions. Rule-based classifiers such as XGBoost often achieve stronger performance on structured features, but they are limited in capturing smooth functional relationships among variables. Similarly, neural network models can represent complex nonlinear interactions but frequently suffer from overfitting and generalization issues. To address these limitations, we propose LFS-FRAME, a Leakage-Free Stacked ensemble framework that integrates functional learning using Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) and rule-based learning via XGBoost for robust multiclass classification.
  The proposed framework constructs unbiased meta-features by employing a strict out-of-fold stacking strategy to ensure complete isolation between training and validation data hence preventing performance leakage. By learning over probabilistic outputs from heterogeneous base learners, the meta-classifier effectively exploits both global functional patterns and sharp decision boundaries present in the complex data. Experimental evaluations on multi-class datasets demonstrate that LFS-FRAME improves performance metrics, and overall accuracy is 89.85% in identifying major families and 81.74% in identifying sub-families relative to strong single-model baselines. These results highlight the effectiveness of leakage-free functional and rule-based stacking for reliable and generalizable multiclass classification.

</details>


### [101] [Pretraining EHR Foundation Models with Patient-Aware Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22114)
*Joshua Placidi,Yuxuan Liu,Jinpei Han,Marek Rei,A. Aldo Faisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Patient Sampling方法替代传统Global Stream方式构建自回归EHR基础模型的训练序列，通过控制患者级别的训练信号分布，在MIMIC-IV数据集上提升了Macro AUROC和AUPRC指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归EHR基础模型沿袭语言模型的预训练方式，将患者轨迹拼接成单一token流并从中采样窗口，这会导致窗口可能混合多个患者，且记录较长的患者贡献更多优化更新，引入偏倚。作者旨在解决这一训练信号分布不均的问题。

Method: 提出Patient Sampling序列构建方法，允许控制训练信号在患者间的分布方式；与传统的Global Stream方法进行对比，采用随机带可控权重的Patient Sampling策略。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV v2.2和v3.1数据集的下游临床任务上，Patient Sampling在Macro AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于Global Stream基线。

Conclusion: 训练和验证序列的构建方式是自回归EHR基础模型中重要但此前未被充分探索的设计选择，Patient Sampling能有效提升模型性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive foundation models for electronic health records (EHRs) typically inherit pretraining methods from language modeling, where patient trajectories are concatenated into a single token stream and windows are sampled from that stream. In EHR data, this choice is consequential: windows may mix multiple patients, and patients with longer records contribute more optimization updates, potentially introducing bias. We propose Patient Sampling, a pretraining sequence-construction method that allows us to control how training signal is distributed across patients. We compare this method to the standard approach, which we refer to as Global Stream. We show that stochastic Patient Sampling with controllable weighting improves performance on real-world EHR data. Across downstream clinical tasks on MIMIC-IV v2.2 and v3.1, Patient Sampling improves Macro AUROC and AUPRC over the Global Stream baseline. These results identify training and validation sequence construction as important and underexplored design choices for autoregressive EHR foundation models.

</details>


### [102] [TriGlue: a Biology-Inspired Generative Model for Generating Molecular Glue-Induced Ternary Complex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22143)
*Yuliang Yan,Shuo Yan,Haochun Tang,Yiqin Sun,Enyan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TriGlue的生物学启发式生成框架，将分子胶设计形式化为三元复合物生成问题，通过界面估计和界面条件复合物生成两个耦合阶段，实现分子胶的从头设计与三元复合物组装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子胶降解剂通过诱导E3泛素连接酶与靶蛋白之间的三元复合物形成实现靶向蛋白降解，具有重要治疗潜力。然而，分子胶的计算设计尚未被充分探索，传统基于结构的药物设计方法不适用，因为分子胶设计受未知蛋白质-蛋白质界面控制，需要同时建模配体生成、蛋白质对接和三元复合物组装，挑战极大。

Method: TriGlue将分子胶设计形式化为三元复合物生成问题，分解为两个耦合阶段：1) SE(3)-等变界面估计模块，从未结合单体结构预测几何约束的蛋白质-蛋白质界面；2) 界面条件三元流匹配网络，联合生成分子胶并预测组装三元复合物所需的刚体变换。

Result: 大量实验表明，TriGlue能够生成化学有效的分子，并产生合理的二元复合物结构，验证了生物学启发式生成建模在加速分子胶发现方面的潜力。

Conclusion: TriGlue证明了生物学启发的生成建模方法能够有效解决分子胶设计这一复杂问题，为加速分子胶降解剂的发现提供了新的计算途径。

Abstract: Molecular glue degraders have emerged as a promising strategy for targeted protein degradation by inducing ternary complex formation between an E3 ubiquitin ligase and a target protein. Despite their therapeutic potential, computational design of molecular glues remains largely unexplored. Unlike conventional structure-based drug design, molecular glue design is governed by the unknown protein-protein interface and requires the simultaneous modeling of ligand generation, protein-protein docking, and ternary complex assembly. In this work, we formulate molecular glue design as a ternary complex generation problem and propose a biology-inspired generative framework, TriGlue. Motivated by the mechanism of molecular glue action, we decompose ternary complex generation into two coupled stages: interface estimation and interface-conditioned complex generation. First, we develop an SE(3)-equivariant interface estimation module that predicts a geometrically constrained protein-protein interface from unbound monomer structures. Second, we introduce an interface-conditioned ternary flow matching network that jointly generates the molecular glue and predicts the rigid-body transformation required to assemble the ternary complex. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TriGlue generates chemically valid molecules and produces plausible ternary complexes, which highlight the potential of biology-inspired generative modeling for accelerating molecular glue discovery. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/molecular-glue-design-806B.

</details>


### [103] [Unbiased Open World Regularization for Fair Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22149)
*L{é}o Nicollier,Marc Pic,Pablo Mus{é},Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UOWReg框架，通过从全局分布匹配转向条件分布匹配，实现表示与目标属性的统计独立性，有效缓解自监督学习中的偏差纠缠问题，并在CelebA基准上降低均衡几率违规，同时引入合成刻印任务验证其防止子种群坍塌的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL和JEPA模型依赖正则化防止表示坍塌，但全局约束（如多元高斯或球面均匀分布）不足以消除偏差纠缠，任务无关特征仍能分割潜在空间。EnD和FSCL等方法虽能经验性去偏，但仅部分近似条件分布匹配，缺乏显式保证。

Method: 提出Unbiased Open World Regularization (UOWReg)，一种仅编码器框架，从全局目标分布约束转向条件分布匹配目标，显式强制学习表示与目标属性之间的统计独立性；在高斯和球面两种潜在空间中使用统计度量实现条件分布约束。

Result: 条件球面均匀分布相比条件高斯分布在线性探针分类中误差更低；UOWReg在CelebA基准上降低均衡几率违规，同时保持与现有仅编码器基线相当的分分类准确率；在合成刻印任务中有效防止子种群坍塌，成功分离微签名。

Conclusion: 从全局到条件的分布匹配转变能保证表示与目标属性的统计独立性；条件球面均匀分布在实际应用中更优；UOWReg在去偏效果和分类性能之间取得良好平衡，并能解决标准SSL中存在的子种群坍塌问题。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, self-supervised learning (SSL) models and Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) remain susceptible to learning spurious biases in the dataset. These techniques rely on regularization, which prevents representation collapse by enforcing a global target distribution such as a multivariate Gaussian or a uniform distribution on the sphere. However, these global constraints are insufficient to prevent bias entanglement, as task-irrelevant features can still segregate the latent space into distinct sub-regions. While recent approaches like Entangling and Disentangling (EnD) and Fair Supervised Contrastive Learning (FSCL) empirically debias the latent space, we show that they act as partial approximations of conditional distribution matching. To enforce this matching explicitly, we propose Unbiased Open World Regularization (UOWReg), an encoder-only framework. We show that this shift from a global to a conditional objective guarantees statistical independence between the learned representations and the targeted attributes, regardless of the chosen target distribution. We empirically validate this framework across both Gaussian and spherical latent spaces, using statistical measures to enforce these target distributions. While conditional matching successfully mitigates bias with both distributions, we demonstrate that enforcing conditional uniformity on the sphere yields a lower linearprobing classification error. Empirically, UOWReg reduces Equalized Odds violations on the CelebA benchmark while maintaining competitive classification accuracy compared to existing encoder-only baselines. Furthermore, we introduce the Synthetic Engraving Task-a novel setting in which a dominant macro-structure masks a fine-grained micro-signature. We show that UOWReg effectively prevents the subpopulation collapse observed in standard SSL, successfully isolating micro-signatures even when heavily entangled with the global structure.

</details>


### [104] [Latent PDE mapping for efficient physics-informed learning across geometries with limited data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22215)
*Ingvild Askim Adde,Mary M. Maleckar,Gabriel Balaban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜变量PDE映射方法，通过变形梯度将几何特定的PDE残差和边界条件映射到预定义的潜变量几何上，实现了基于物理信息的机器学习模型在稀疏训练数据下的高效几何泛化。在心脏电生理学的Aliev-Panfilov PDE上验证，仅用15个几何样本训练，在某些几何族上实现了约4-6倍的相对L2误差降低，且计算开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统物理信息机器学习方法在处理不同几何形状时缺乏几何一致性形状梯度的问题，实现在稀疏训练数据下的高效几何泛化。

Method: 提出潜变量PDE映射（latent PDE mapping），利用变形梯度将几何特定的PDE残差和边界条件拉回到预定义的潜变量几何上，自动计算几何一致的形状梯度。

Result: 在仿射和剪切变形参数化的几何上改进适中，但在特定几何族上实现了约4-6倍的均方相对L2误差降低；训练计算成本适中，推理时计算成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 潜变量PDE映射能够从有限的训练几何样本中创建可泛化的物理信息机器学习模型，是一种广泛适用的物理学-informed学习技术。

Abstract: In this study, we introduce latent PDE mapping, a broadly applicable physics-informed learning technique designed to enable efficient geometric generalization with sparse training data. Latent PDE mapping pulls back geometry-specific PDE residuals and boundary conditions to a predefined latent geometry via the deformation gradient, thereby enabling the automated calculation of geometry-consistent shape gradients that are missing in conventional physics-informed machine learning formulations. We demonstrate the utility of latent PDE mapping in solving the anisotropic Aliev-Panfilov PDE of cardiac electrophysiology using both physics-informed neural networks and physics-informed deep operator networks. The Aliev-Panfilov PDE serves as a challenging exemplar: a nonlinear, time-dependent PDE benchmark with sharp gradients that are expensive to capture using traditional numerical solvers. To represent the limited data regime, we train the networks using just fifteen geometric samples drawn from parameterized distributions in two and three spatial dimensions. While modest improvements appear for geometries parameterized by affine and shear deformations, latent PDE mapping demonstrates significant benefits on select geometric families, achieving a factor ~4-6 reduction in mean relative L2 error. Furthermore, our results show that the computational cost of applying latent PDE mapping was modest during network training, and negligible at inference. Taken together, our study highlights how latent PDE mapping facilitates the creation of generalizable physics-informed machine learning models from limited sets of training geometries.

</details>


### [105] [Optimization of time-consuming experimental conditions using pseudo-experimental data guided by adaptive polynomial regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22238)
*Hirotaka Sugawara,Yujin Taguchi,Kei Minagawa,Yusuke Hiki,Takashi Morikura,Akira Funahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolyBO方法，通过自适应更新的低容量多项式回归模型生成高质量伪实验数据，结合真实与伪数据更新贝叶斯优化代理模型，在合成基准函数中优化时间中位数减少42%，在真实材料成分优化问题中减少96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化在实验科学中收敛性差，且当实验数据有限时，现有的伪实验数据生成方法质量不足，导致优化效率低下。

Method: PolyBO采用低容量多项式回归模型自适应更新生成高质量伪实验数据，将实验数据与伪实验数据合并后更新BO代理模型，进而执行优化。

Result: 在合成基准函数上优化时间中位数减少42%；在真实材料成分优化问题中优化时间中位数减少96%。

Conclusion: PolyBO在单次实验耗时长的场景下实现了高效优化，显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is an optimization method that sequentially proposes the next candidate explainable variables for optimizing target variables by balancing exploration and exploitation. BO is often used under a limited evaluation budget, such as hyperparameter tuning of deep learning. Despite its effectiveness, conventional BO may have poor convergence in practical experimental science where each evaluation is often costly and time-consuming. Recently, BO methods have been proposed that accelerate optimization by using pseudo-experimental data that simulate experimental data. However, when only a limited number of experimental data are available, the generated pseudo-experimental data may be of insufficient quality. In this study, we developed PolyBO to improve optimization time by generating high-quality pseudo-experimental data even when the number of trials is limited. PolyBO performs BO efficiently by generating pseudo-experimental data with an adaptively updated versatile parametric model. This low-capacity polynomial regression model is intended to enable efficient BO even with limited experimental data. PolyBO updates the BO surrogate model with a combined dataset consisting of experimental data and pseudo-experimental data and then performs optimization. Using synthetic benchmark functions with diverse landscapes, we found that PolyBO reduced the optimization time by a median of 42\%. For a real-world material composition optimization problem, PolyBO reduced the optimization time by a median of 96\% compared with conventional methods. Overall, PolyBO achieves efficient optimization in settings where each experiment requires a long time.

</details>


### [106] [IFCLoRA: Topology-Aware Rank Allocation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22251)
*Wei Zhang,Xinwu Liu,Yihang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IFCLoRA提出了一种在微调前基于信息流中心性进行拓扑感知的秩分配方法，通过小规模校准集和冻结预训练模型构建任务条件交互图，结合全局信息流先验与局部梯度敏感性计算每个模块的适应重要性，在固定总秩预算下实现优于LoRA、AdaLoRA和EVA的性能，且训练成本与标准LoRA相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应秩方法（如AdaLoRA）依赖训练过程中的局部梯度统计来分配秩，这不仅引入额外的内存和计算开销，还忽略了任务条件的全局信息流，导致秩分配可能偏离全局最优。

Method: IFCLoRA在微调前执行：1) 利用小规模校准集和冻结预训练模型构建稀疏的任务条件交互图，节点为LoRA兼容模块；2) 结合全局信息流拓扑先验与局部梯度敏感性，计算信息流中心性分数，估计每个模块在多跳传播下的适应重要性；3) 在全局预算下一次性分配秩。

Result: 在多个模型、任务和低秩设置下，IFCLoRA一致优于LoRA、AdaLoRA和EVA；在LLaMA 3 8B数学推理任务上，秩4时提升1.36%，秩8时提升1.82%。

Conclusion: 全局信息流结构为低预算参数高效微调提供了信息丰富且可解释的先验，任务依赖的非均匀秩分布进一步验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large language models, but its performance depends strongly on how a fixed rank budget is distributed across Transformer modules. Existing adaptive-rank methods usually rely on local gradient statistics collected during training, which introduces extra memory and computation and overlooks task-conditioned global information flow. We propose IFCLoRA, a topology-aware rank allocation method applied before fine-tuning. Using a small calibration set and a frozen pretrained model, IFCLoRA builds a sparse task-conditioned interaction graph whose nodes represent LoRA-compatible modules. It combines a global information-flow topology prior with local gradient sensitivity to compute Information-Flow Centrality scores, which estimate each module's adaptation importance under multi-hop propagation. Ranks are then assigned once under a global budget. Across multiple models, tasks, and low-rank settings, IFCLoRA consistently outperforms LoRA, AdaLoRA, and EVA under matched training configurations and total rank budgets, while retaining training costs comparable to standard LoRA. On mathematical reasoning with LLaMA 3 8B, IFCLoRA improves over LoRA by 1.36 percent at rank 4 and 1.82 percent at rank 8. Further analysis shows task-dependent, non-uniform rank profiles, indicating that global information-flow structure provides an informative and interpretable prior for low-budget parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [107] [Class-Balanced Softmax: A Bayes Theory-Based Method for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22258)
*Yi-Hang Zhu,Rajeev Raman,Shiqi Su,Jianyuan Sun,Xinyu Yang,Nan Xing,Huiyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出类别平衡Softmax（CBS）方法，通过理论贝叶斯框架和幂律假设进行logit调整，有效解决了不平衡数据集上传统softmax和Balanced Softmax的性能退化问题，并首次定义了"偏好问题"及其量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统softmax分类器在不平衡数据集上性能显著下降，而现有最优方法Balanced Softmax存在固有缺陷（如尾部类别测试准确率过低），亟需一种更优的再平衡方法。

Method: 提出Class-Balanced Softmax (CBS)，基于理论贝叶斯框架和启发式幂律假设设计简单的logit调整方法，计算成本低且易于集成；同时定义并量化了不平衡训练中的"偏好问题"（模型对数据有限类别表现出更高训练误差和更大泛化差距），并引入新指标进行度量。

Result: 在大型基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，CBS具有高度可扩展性，在性能上优于包括Balanced Softmax在内的现有方法。

Conclusion: CBS通过理论驱动的简单logit调整有效缓解了不平衡学习中的偏好问题，是一种计算高效且可扩展的先进再平衡方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models using traditional softmax classifiers have achieved remarkable success in various classification tasks. However, their performance degrades significantly on imbalanced datasets. Although Balanced Softmax is widely adopted as a state-of-the-art rebalancing method, it possesses inherent limitations, such as yielding disproportionately lower testing accuracy for tail classes. To mitigate these shortcomings, we propose the Class-Balanced Softmax (CBS). Rooted in a theoretical Bayesian framework and a heuristic power-law assumption, the CBS is a simple logit adjustment that is computationally inexpensive and easily integrated into existing pipelines. Furthermore, we characterise a fundamental phenomenon in models trained on imbalanced data, termed the preference issue, wherein models exhibit higher training error and a larger generalisation gap for classes with limited data. To quantify this issue, we introduce a novel metric and demonstrate that CBS effectively mitigates the preference issue. Extensive experiments on large-scale benchmarks show that CBS is highly scalable and outperforms existing methods, including Balanced Softmax.

</details>


### [108] [Autoregressive EHR Foundation Models with Multimodal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22264)
*Yuxuan Liu,Joshua Placidi,Jinpei Han,Alfred John Balston,Marek Rei,A. Aldo Faisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态融合框架，通过潜压缩和门控交叉注意力机制，将ECG波形、胸部X光影像和临床笔记等辅助模态条件化到自回归EHR基础模型上，实现零样本临床预测。实验表明，最佳潜压缩配置优于无压缩和均值池化，更强的预训练编码器效果更好，但单纯添加辅助模态不保证性能提升，需要精心设计融合架构和临床评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归基础模型大多仅基于结构化事件编码训练，未能以合理方式整合多种模态数据（如ECG、影像、文本），限制了在临床预测中的表达能力。

Method: 提出多模态条件化框架：对每种辅助模态使用潜压缩（latent compression）降维，再通过门控交叉注意力（gated cross-attention）结合时间对齐，将多模态信息注入自回归EHR模型。研究了压缩策略和预训练编码器选择两个关键设计因素。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV上，最佳潜压缩配置优于无压缩交叉注意力和均值池化；更强的预训练编码器在同模态内持续优于弱编码器；但添加辅助模态不保证ICU死亡率预测优于纯EHR基线。

Conclusion: 多模态融合需要精心设计融合架构和临床上下文评估，才能发挥辅助模态的增益效果。

Abstract: Autoregressive foundation models trained on tokenized electronic health records (EHRs) can support zero-shot clinical prediction, yet most operate on structured event codes alone, and do not incorporate multiple modalities in a principled way. We present a framework for conditioning such models on auxiliary clinical modalities, including ECG waveforms, chest X-ray images, and clinical notes, using modality-specific latent compression and gated cross-attention with temporal alignment. We investigate two key design choices: (1) how to compress long per-modality sequences (e.g., ECG time series) before they enter the multi-modal cross-attention. This feature may be essential to reduce compute overheads and may be beneficial for generalization; (2) how the choice of pretrained encoder for each modality impacts downstream performance. Through controlled ablations on MIMIC-IV, we show that the best latent-compression configurations outperforms both uncompressed cross-attention and mean pooling. Encoder choice has a clear within-modality effect, with stronger pretrained encoders consistently outperforming weaker alternatives. We further show that merely adding auxiliary modalities does not guarantee improvement on ICU mortality prediction over an EHR-only baseline. This implies that careful design of the fusion architecture and an appropriate evaluation in the clinical context are required.

</details>


### [109] [Efficient Recommendations via Graph Coarsening and Label Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22287)
*Alessandro Sbandi,Federico Siciliano,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合图粗化与两阶段扩散的推荐框架，在电信领域实现大规模图上效率与质量的平衡，相比全图LPA基线，NDCG@5提升最高达50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级图推荐系统面临图规模庞大带来的可扩展性和效率挑战，亟需能在预测质量与计算成本之间取得平衡的技术。

Method: 提出灵活的两阶段扩散框架：1) 利用领域启发式方法将节点聚合为社区进行图粗化；2) 第一阶段在粗化图上用LPA或GNN传播标签获得粗粒度预测；3) 第二阶段在子图上用LPA为个体用户生成最终推荐。

Result: 在真实电信数据集上，双LPA方案NDCG@5提升24%；引入轻量GNN后提升超50%，但训练和推理时间显著增加。

Conclusion: 图粗化驱动的两阶段扩散方法能在可扩展性、延迟和推荐质量间取得最优平衡。

Abstract: Graph-based recommendations are widely adopted in real-world industrial applications. However, graphs in these systems often reach a massive scale, posing notable scalability and efficiency challenges. This requires techniques that can effectively balance predictive quality with computational cost. One promising approach is graph coarsening, an adaptive graph reduction technique that offers a way to systematically construct smaller, yet structurally representative, versions of the original large-scale graphs.
  In this work, we propose a flexible two-stage diffusion framework that combines graph coarsening with multi-step label propagation in the telecommunications domain. Domain-specific heuristics are applied to first aggregate nodes into meaningful communities, reducing graph size while preserving essential business-relevant relationships. An initial diffusion process done by a Label Propagation Algorithm (LPA) or a Graph Neural Network (GNN) propagates labels across the coarsened graph to produce coarse-grained predictions. Finally, a second LPA within subgraphs generates the final recommendations for individual users.
  On a real-world telecommunications dataset, when using LPA in both stages, our method achieves up to +24% NDCG@5 over the full-graph LPA baseline. Incorporating a lightweight GNN in the first stage further boosts NDCG@5 by more than 50%, but requires substantial training and inference time. Through extensive experiments and a detailed ablation, we quantify these trade-offs and demonstrate that our coarsening-driven approach delivers an optimal balance between scalability, latency, and recommendation quality.

</details>


### [110] [Synthetic Speech, Real Signal: Paralinguistic Preservation and Cross-Lingual Augmentation via Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22304)
*Roseline Polle,Owen Parsons,George Fairs,Luis Miguel San Martin Fernandez,Cole Looney,Xiaoliang Wu,Alexandra Livia Georgescu,Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了语音克隆在副语言任务（尤其是临床语音分析）中的数据增强效果，发现多数语音克隆模型能较好地保留副语言信号，且将英语临床语音克隆为日语后，在抑郁和焦虑检测任务上优于直接跨语言迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 副语言任务（尤其是临床任务）中标注数据昂贵且部分患者群体代表性不足，而语音数据增强在副语言领域研究较少，现有语音克隆评估多关注可懂度和说话人相似度，未验证是否保留副语言信号。

Method: 在公开和临床数据集上，对8种语音克隆模型在5项副语言任务上进行基准测试；并将英语临床语音克隆为日语，在真实日语语音上进行抑郁和焦虑检测，与原始跨语言迁移进行对比。

Result: 多数语音克隆模型能保留副语言信号，仅有适度退化；克隆数据训练的模型在抑郁和焦虑检测上优于直接跨语言迁移。

Conclusion: 语音克隆是增强低资源语言临床语音数据的有前景方向。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation in speech is common practice for linguistic tasks like ASR, but has seen far less work for paralinguistic ones, especially clinical tasks where labelled data is expensive and some patient groups are underrepresented. Voice cloning is one such augmentation approach, but is typically evaluated on speech intelligibility (WER) or speaker similarity (SS) rather than on downstream performance, and it remains unclear whether these preserve the paralinguistic signal such tasks depend on. We benchmark eight voice cloning models on five paralinguistic tasks across public and clinical datasets, showing most preserve signal with modest degradation. We then clone English clinical speech into Japanese and find that training on cloned data outperforms raw cross-lingual transfer for depression and anxiety detection on real Japanese speech, suggesting voice cloning is a promising direction for augmenting clinical speech data in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [111] [Evolution-Aware MSA Reasoning for Subsampling via Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22314)
*Zhangzhi Xiong,Minzhang Li,Haotian Yu,Sixian Shen,Kexin Zhang,Mingrui Li,Jie Zheng,Kewei Tu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AP-REASONER，将MSA子采样重新定义为可控制的目标优化问题，利用基于亲和传播的因子图方法，通过消息传递推理实现固定预算下的MSA子集选择，在长程接触预测和构象集合预测任务上优于现有启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSA子采样策略（随机选择、基于同源性过滤、多样性驱动采样）虽然有效，但无法对子集中保留的进化信号进行精细控制，缺乏可解释性和可调节性。

Method: 将MSA子采样形式化为显式优化问题，引入进化感知的单一因子、样本一致性因子及两个控制旋钮，采用亲和传播（Affinity Propagation）构建因子图，通过消息传递机制推理出固定预算的MSA子集。

Result: 在长程接触预测和构象集合预测任务上，AP-REASONER优于基线子采样方法，并实现对替代蛋白质构象的可控恢复。

Conclusion: 将MSA子采样建模为可控优化问题具有重要价值，因子图推理为启发式选择提供了有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Multiple Sequence Alignments (MSAs) provide protein language models with explicit evolutionary context, but their large depth makes subsampling unavoidable under limited token budgets. Existing strategies, including random selection, identity-based filtering, and diversity-driven sampling, are effective heuristics, yet provide limited control over the evolutionary signals retained in the subset. In this work, we recast MSA subsampling as an explicit optimization problem, where key evolutionary measures, including query identity and diversity, are treated as controllable objectives. Building on this view, we introduce AP-REASONER, an Affinity-Propagation-based factor-graph approach. With evolution-aware unary factors, exemplar-consistency factors, and two control knobs, AP-REASONER performs factor-graph reasoning through message passing to infer a fixed-budget MSA subset. Experiments on long-range contact prediction and conformational ensemble prediction show that AP-REASONER outperforms baseline subsamplers on structure-sensitive downstream tasks and enables controllable recovery of alternative protein conformations. These results highlight the value of modeling MSA subsampling as a controllable optimization problem, where factor-graph reasoning offers an effective alternative to heuristic selection.

</details>


### [112] [Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation via Byte-Prefix Marginalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22334)
*Hao Wang,Kun Yuan,Wenlin Zhong,Minglei Zhang,Han Xiao,Ming Sun,Honggang Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Byte-Prefix Marginalization (BPM)方法，通过在共享字节空间重新表达教师模型的下一词元分布，实现跨不同分词器的知识蒸馏，在数学和编程基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同开放权重语言模型具有互补能力，但现有跨分词器蒸馏方法要么丢弃教师概率质量，要么将概率分配给语义不相关的学生词元，无法有效实现知识迁移。

Method: BPM方法将教师词元概率映射到学生词元空间：将每个教师词元的概率分配给字节表示为其前缀的最长学生词元，聚合映射到同一学生词元的概率质量，将未匹配的质量放入显式残差类别。当相关前缀不跨多个教师词元时（99%以上训练位置满足），精确恢复教师诱导的字节前缀边际分布；否则使用质量保持的链式分解下界。

Result: 以Qwen3-32B、GLM-Z1-9B-0414和MiniMax-M2.7为教师模型，在六个数学和编程基准上，BPM一致优于所有当前跨分词器方法，六基准avg@8提升3.7-6.6个点。

Conclusion: BPM实现了词汇表完整、字节对齐、质量保持的目标分布，有效解决了跨分词器知识蒸馏中的概率分配问题。

Abstract: Open-weight language models from different families exhibit complementary capabilities, motivating their consolidation into a compact student through on-policy distillation (OPD). However, full-vocabulary OPD typically assumes a shared tokenizer, while existing cross-tokenizer methods may discard teacher probability mass or assign it to student tokens with unrelated content. We introduce Byte-Prefix Marginalization (BPM), which re-expresses the teacher's next-token distribution over the student vocabulary in a shared byte space. Specifically, BPM assigns each teacher token's probability to the longest student token whose byte representation is a prefix of the teacher token's bytes, aggregates mass mapped to the same student token, and places otherwise unmatched mass in an explicit residual category. This produces a vocabulary-complete, byte-aligned, and mass-preserving target for dense OPD. The target exactly recovers the teacher-induced byte-prefix marginal when the relevant prefix does not span multiple teacher tokens (a condition satisfied at more than 99% of training positions) and uses a mass-preserving, chain-factorized lower bound otherwise. Across Qwen3-32B, GLM-Z1-9B-0414, and MiniMax-M2.7 as teachers, BPM consistently outperforms current cross-tokenizer methods on six mathematics and programming benchmarks, improving six-benchmark avg@8 by 3.7-6.6 points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond Binary Rooftop Mapping: A Four-Class Deep Learning Framework for Green Roof Potential Assessment from Open Swiss Geospatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22342)
*Htet Yamin Ko Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于改进深度卷积神经网络的屋顶分类框架，结合高分辨率航拍影像和屋顶坡度信息（源自数字表面模型），利用完全公开的Swisstopo数据集，将屋顶分为四类：现有绿屋顶、适宜绿化屋顶、光伏板屋顶和不适宜绿化的平屋顶。该框架在瑞士伯尔尼应用，为城市绿色基础设施部署提供证据支持，且完全开源可迁移至其他城市。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究要么仅测绘现有绿屋顶，要么仅测绘有绿化潜力的屋顶，缺乏同时兼顾两者的综合分类框架，难以支持城市热岛缓减的生态服务评估和气候适应策略制定。

Method: 基于改进的Roofpedia深度卷积神经网络分类框架，融合高分辨率航空影像（SWISSIMAGE）和从数字表面模型（swissSURFACE3D）中提取的屋顶坡度信息，使用swissTLM3D建筑足迹数据，在瑞士伯尔尼对屋顶进行四分类：现有绿屋顶、适宜绿化屋顶、光伏板屋顶、不适宜绿化的平屋顶。框架完全基于公开的Swisstopo数据集且开源。

Result: 成功将伯尔尼屋顶分为四类，识别出现有绿屋顶及可扩展绿化的现实机会，为城市绿色基础设施部署提供了基于证据的空间信息。

Conclusion: 该框架能同时识别现有绿屋顶和适宜绿化屋顶，为城市气候适应策略提供全面空间信息；完全开源且依赖公开数据，具有良好的可迁移性，可推广至瑞士其他城市乃至全球城市。

Abstract: The development of effective urban climate adaptation strategies requires comprehensive spatial information on rooftops and buildings, since such information underpins the assessment of ecosystem services provided by green infrastructure, particularly for urban heat island (UHI) mitigation. Although green roofs are widely acknowledged as a promising measure for improving urban thermal comfort, most existing research maps either current green rooftops or rooftops with greening potential, but not both. This study presents a modified deep convolutional neural network rooftop classification framework based on Roofpedia, developed by the Urban Analytics Lab at the National University of Singapore. The proposed model combines high resolution aerial imagery with rooftop slope information derived from a digital surface model and relies entirely on publicly available Swisstopo datasets: SWISSIMAGE orthophotos, swissSURFACE3D elevation data, and swissTLM3D building footprints. Applied to Bern, Switzerland, the model labels rooftops into four categories: existing green roofs, rooftops suitable for green roof installation, rooftops with solar panels, and flat rooftops unsuitable for greening. The framework identifies realistic opportunities for green roof expansion and supplies urban planners with evidence-based information for green infrastructure deployment in Bern and other Swiss cities. Because it is fully open source, the framework is transferable to cities worldwide.

</details>


### [114] [IQ-JEPA: A Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with a Hermitian Vision Transformer for Sound Speed and Attenuation Estimation from Ultrasound IQ Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22351)
*Masashi Sode,Gianmarco Pinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IQ-JEPA，一种用于超声脉冲回波数据中声速重建的自监督学习方法。通过预测掩码IQ区域的潜在表征进行无标签预训练，再在模拟数据上微调，利用Hermitian视觉变换器处理复信号，实现了显著优于监督学习的标签效率（10,000标签时15.60 m/s，标签效率提升约3倍），并展现出良好的跨分布迁移能力，是定量超声基础模型的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始脉冲回波通道数据中恢复声速是一个非线性逆问题。学习型求解器速度快但需要大量标签，而模拟声速标签计算成本高昂，真实通道数据虽然丰富却缺乏标签。因此，需要一种能同时利用有标签模拟数据和无标签真实数据的方法，以提高标签效率。

Method: 提出IQ-JEPA框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 无标签预训练：编码器通过预测掩码IQ区域的潜在表征来学习，利用声速在IQ信号中表现为相位差（对恒定相位偏移不变）的特性；(2) 有监督微调：在模拟声速图上微调。编码器采用Hermitian视觉变换器，其注意力机制对相位等变、共轭乘积前馈网络对相位不变，从而读取与经典相干方法类似的物理量。

Result: 在79,293个Fullwave 2.5模拟数据（2.5 MHz）上，对63,435个无标签数据进行预训练后，10,000个标签时达到15.60 m/s的误差，标签效率约为监督学习的3倍，1,000个标签时超过4倍。与InversionNet基线相比约低2.2倍，全标签时误差为8.71 m/s。增益随无标签预训练数据增加而增长，且编码器具有良好的跨分布迁移能力。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练是提升标签效率的主导因素。同一编码器可迁移至其他任务，其冻结特征能揭示声速和衰减，跨分布（分层体模与腹部体模）预训练精度损失很小。该工作被视为迈向定量超声基础模型的第一步。

Abstract: The speed of sound in tissue is a prerequisite for well-focused imaging and has diagnostic value, but recovering it from raw pulse-echo channel data is fundamentally a nonlinear inverse problem. Learned solvers are fast yet label hungry. Simulated sound-speed labels are expensive, while abundant real channel data is unlabeled. We propose IQ-JEPA to exploit both data types. An encoder is pretrained without labels to predict the latent representation of masked in-phase and quadrature (IQ) regions from visible context, then fine-tuned on simulated maps. Sound speed appears in the IQ signal as a phase difference, invariant to the constant phase offset. The encoder is a Hermitian vision transformer that operates on the complex signal directly. Its attention is equivariant to that phase and its conjugate-product feed-forward is invariant to it, so the encoder reads a quantity analogous to the one classical coherence methods use. On 79,293 Fullwave 2.5 simulations at 2.5 MHz, pretraining on the 63,435 unlabeled acquisitions reaches 15.60 m/s at 10,000 labels. This is a roughly threefold gain in label efficiency over supervised training, growing to over fourfold at 1,000 labels. It is about 2.2x below an InversionNet baseline, and 8.71 m/s at full labels. The gain still grows with more unlabeled pretraining data. Our comparisons point to self-supervision as the dominant factor. The same encoder transfers. Its frozen features expose sound speed and attenuation, and cross-distribution pretraining between layered and abdominal phantoms costs little accuracy. We see this as a first step toward a foundation model for quantitative ultrasound.

</details>


### [115] [Integrated Order Dispatching and Routing for Last-Mile Pickup via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22356)
*Yida Xu,Zhaofang Mao,Yuheng Miao,Jiaxin Zhang,Yiting Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成优化框架，将学习的路由预言机与实时调度启发式方法相结合，以解决最后一公里取件中订单调度与路径规划相互耦合的难题。该框架使用动态残差图注意力网络编码器与前瞻性快递员个性化解码器处理路径子问题，并设计了路由预言机引导的调度启发式与局部搜索处理调度子问题。在菜鸟物流真实数据集上的离线评估与在线滚动时域仿真实验表明，该方法在解质量和求解时间上均优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最后一公里取件业务日益复杂，订单调度与路径规划两个决策过程紧密耦合。分开求解忽略了二者的相互依赖性，而完全端到端学习在大规模变尺度实例上因奖励稀疏而不稳定且成本高，亟需一种既能捕获耦合关系又能保持实时可扩展性的集成优化方法。

Method: 提出集成优化框架：(1) 路径子问题：设计动态残差图注意力网络编码器 + 前瞻性快递员个性化解码器作为学习的路由预言机；(2) 调度子问题：开发路由预言机引导的调度启发式算法，结合局部搜索，由预言机提供近优解来筛选候选快递员，同时保持实时可扩展性。

Result: 在菜鸟物流真实数据集上进行了离线评估和在线滚动时域仿真，实验结果表明该方法在解质量和求解时间上均优于其他基准方法。

Conclusion: 该集成优化框架能有效支持物流企业解决实时、大规模的最后一公里取件问题，兼顾了路径优化与调度决策的耦合性及实时可扩展性。

Abstract: In recent years, the growing complexity of last-mile pickup operations has increased the need for fast and accurate decision-making on logistics platforms. This challenge is fundamentally driven by two key and tightly coupled decision-making processes: order dispatching and routing. Solving them separately overlooks their interdependence, while fully end-to-end learning can be unstable and costly on large, variable-scale instances due to sparse rewards. To solve this problem, we propose an integrated optimization framework which couples a learned routing oracle with real-time dispatching heuristics. For the routing subproblem, we develop a Dynamic-Residual Graph Attention Network encoder with a Look-Ahead Courier-Personalized decoder. For the dispatching subproblem, we develop a routing-oracle-guided dispatching heuristic with local search, where the oracle provides near-optimal solutions to select candidate couriers while retaining real-time scalability. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets from Cainiao Logistics are used to test the performance of our approach, including an offline evaluation and an online rolling-horizon simulation. The experimental results show that our approach outperforms other benchmarks regarding solution quality and solving time, indicating it can effectively support logistics companies in solving real-time and large-scale last-mile pickup problems.

</details>


### [116] [Indexing: the Beginning and the End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22361)
*Alexander Kozachinskiy,Vicente Opazo,Felipe Urrutia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"索引原语"（根据位置索引输出对应比特值）研究现代深度学习架构（RNN、softmax Transformer、线性注意力Transformer和状态空间模型）中的信息瓶颈。作者引入因果复杂度概念，发现低因果复杂度的架构（低参数RNN、SSM、掩码线性注意力Transformer）在索引出现在输入末尾时无法在常数层内解决该任务，而softmax Transformer和非掩码线性注意力Transformer则能解决。当索引出现在开头时，RNN可在1层内解决，其他架构需要2层。所有不可能性结论都是无条件的，适用于无限精度实数运算，且实验（n≤64）与理论定性吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不同深度学习架构在信息瓶颈方面的根本性差异，特别是通过"索引原语"这一简单但信息论上重要的任务来区分各架构的表达能力上限。

Method: 提出"因果复杂度"概念，对掩码架构进行理论分析；通过索引原语任务，将索引放置于输入的不同位置（末尾/开头），分析各架构是否能在常数层内解决该任务；结合实验验证（n≤64）。

Result: 1）低因果复杂度的架构（低参数RNN、SSM、掩码线性注意力Transformer）在索引位于末尾时无法常数层内解决索引任务；2）softmax Transformer可在1层、非掩码线性注意力Transformer可在2层解决；3）索引位于开头时，RNN可在1层解决，其他架构需2层；4）实验与理论定性一致。

Conclusion: 不同架构在处理信息瓶颈时存在根本性能力差异，因果复杂度是刻画这种差异的有效理论工具；掩码机制和注意力类型对架构的信息传递能力有决定性影响。

Abstract: We study information bottlenecks in modern deep-learning architectures -- RNNs, softmax transformers, linear-attention transformers and state-space models -- through the lens of the indexing primitive. In this primitive, the input consists of $n$ bits and one integer $i$ from $1$ to $n$ called the index, and the output equals the value of the $i$-th bit.
  We introduce causal complexity for masked architectures. We show that architectures with low causal complexity cannot solve the indexing primitive in any constant number of layers when the index appears at the end of the input. In particular, this limitation applies to low-parameter RNNs, SSMs and masked linear-attention transformers. In contrast, small softmax transformers can solve it in one layer, while non-masked linear-attention transformers can solve it in 2, which separates them from their masked counterparts. In turn, when the index appears at the beginning, we show that small RNNs are capable of solving this task in 1 layer, while all the other architectures require 2.
  All our impossibility results are unconditional and apply even to models that employ infinite-precision real arithmetic. Moreover, experiments for up to $n=64$ qualitatively align with our theory: configurations with low-parameter theoretical solutions learn the indexing task easily, while configurations that do not admit such theoretical solutions struggle to learn as the sequence length grows.

</details>


### [117] [Interior interpretability with attention rollout: contraction and propagation profiles in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22367)
*Umberto Biccari,Qian Huang,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了“内部可解释性”视角，利用注意力传播（Attention Rollout）分析表格型Transformer的中间层组织，并通过Doeblin-Dobrushin收缩理论证明了传播算子的结构性质，最后在代谢组学年龄预测任务上验证了方法的诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法只能将输入变量与模型输出关联，无法刻画交互算子如何在模型中间层之间组合传播，缺乏对模型内部组织的理解工具。

Method: 提出基于注意力传播（Attention Rollout）的内部可解释性框架，将其解释为行随机算子；利用Doeblin-Dobrushin收缩理论分析传播算子性质，证明当Dobrushin系数较小时，Rollout算子近似于秩为1的随机矩阵。

Result: 实验发现：（1）代谢组学年龄预测Transformer中Rollout收缩随网络深度增强；（2）训练模型与随机初始化模型的传播特征存在差异；（3）Rollout排名变量与PCA及GradientExplainer近似SHAP在顶部变量上有局部一致性，但全局排名一致性较弱。

Conclusion: 注意力传播（Attention Rollout）更适合作为注意力中介传播的诊断工具，而非完整的因果解释或忠实归因方法。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods assign scores relating input variables to a model's output, but do not by themselves characterize how explicitly defined interaction operators compose across its intermediate layers. We introduce \emph{interior interpretability}, a propagation-based perspective on internal model organization, and instantiate it for tabular Transformers using attention rollout. We interpret rollout as a row-stochastic operator encoding attention-mediated propagation between feature tokens. By applying classical Doeblin--Dobrushin contraction theory, we show that a rollout operator with a small Dobrushin coefficient is quantitatively close to a rank-one stochastic matrix whose common row is determined by its normalized column sums. This result gives a structural interpretation to the corresponding rollout propagation profile. In Transformers trained for metabolomic age prediction, the measured rollout contraction strengthens with depth. Trained and randomly initialized models also exhibit different propagation profiles, although the present experiments do not establish the predictive relevance of individual rollout-ranked variables. Exploratory comparisons with PCA and GradientExplainer approximations to SHAP reveal localized agreement among highly ranked variables but weak agreement across complete rankings. Attention rollout is therefore used here as a diagnostic of attention-mediated propagation, not as a causal explanation or faithful attribution of the complete Transformer.

</details>


### [118] [LunarFM: A Shared Multimodal Representation of the Moon's Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22408)
*Marc Girona-Mata,Jakob Gawlikowski,Sumit Goski,Gautier Bardi de Fourtou,Valentin T. Bickel,Ben Moseley,Abigail Calzada-Diaz,Sylvester Kaczmarek,Raúl Ramos-Pollán*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LunarFM，一个多模态基础模型，通过融合来自三个月球任务的六种仪器观测数据（18个输入通道），学习月球表面的通用表征。该模型支持相似性搜索、小样本资源映射、矿物丰度回归和地质单元分类等下游任务，并提供了配套的机器学习数据集和768维联合嵌入表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球探索需求日益增长，但现有的科学分析和资源映射因多仪器观测异构、标签稀疏以及任务特定建模流程而碎片化，缺乏统一的月球表面通用表征。

Method: 提出LunarFM多模态基础模型，采用多模态掩码自编码器架构，将来自三个月球任务六种仪器的18个输入通道映射到共享的768维嵌入空间，并构建了配准的多模态数据集。

Result: 该嵌入空间支持相似性搜索、小样本资源映射、矿物丰度回归和地质单元分类等多种下游任务，实现了高效的科学研究与资源导向分析。

Conclusion: LunarFM通过多模态基础模型整合异构轨道观测数据，为月球表面的大规模、高精度表征提供了统一框架，有助于推动月球原位资源利用和可持续人类存在。

Abstract: The renewed global focus on lunar exploration, driven by the prospect of in-situ resource utilization and a sustained human presence on the Moon, has created growing demand for accurate, large-scale characterization of the lunar surface. Although vast quantities of orbital remote-sensing data have been collected, scientific analysis and resource mapping remain fragmented by heterogeneous multiinstrument observations, sparse labels, and bespoke task-specific modelling workflows. Here we introduce LunarFM, a multimodal foundation model that learns a general representation of the lunar surface from diverse orbital measurements. LunarFM assimilates observations from six instruments across three lunar missions, mapping 18 input channels to a shared embedding space. We demonstrate that this embedding space supports a diverse range of downstream applications, including similarity search, few-shot resource mapping, mineral abundance regression, and geological unit classification, enabling efficient scientific investigation and resource-oriented analysis. We provide a machine-learning-ready dataset of co-registered multimodal observations spanning latitudes from 70°S to 70°N, a pretrained multimodal masked autoencoder, and a companion embedding dataset providing a joint 768-dimensional representation of lunar surface properties. All code and data are available at https://lunarfm.trillium.tech/

</details>


### [119] [On the Identifiability of Controlled World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22430)
*Xiangteng Zhang,Yang Guan,Bo Zhang,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了受控环境下的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的可辨识性理论，证明在满足频谱分离和非退化条件动作变异两个条件时，JEPA目标函数的全局最小值能够识别出潜在状态和受控转移，并量化了近似优化下的辨识边界以及有限动作覆盖带来的预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA在视觉控制和潜在空间规划中表现良好，但其在非线性观测和有限条件动作变异的策略下，能否从受控潜在预测中同时识别出潜在状态和受控动力学的问题尚未解决，状态依赖的演化与动作效应可能存在统计混淆。

Method: 基于高斯潜在状态和高斯行为策略的受控世界模型，建立联合可辨识性理论；提出两种策略依赖条件（可预测信号的频谱分离、非退化条件动作变异）；推导近似优化下的定量辨识界；构建弱激发动作方向上的预测器扰动，分析其反事实-在策略误差比。

Result: 证明了在两种条件均满足时，JEPA目标函数的全局最小值可在正交变换意义下识别潜在状态和受控转移；给出了近似优化下表示和转移可辨识性的定量界；发现逆转移可辨识性边际决定了有限动作覆盖的成本。实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上解释了JEPA在受控环境中能够辨识潜在状态和动力学机制的条件，揭示了动作覆盖范围对反事实预测和潜在规划的关键影响，为设计更鲁棒的世界模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning world models that infer environment dynamics from high-dimensional observations and predict outcomes under candidate actions is central to planning and control. Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provide a compelling framework for learning such models in representation space. Recent action-conditioned extensions perform promisingly in visual control and latent-space planning, but leave a fundamental question unresolved: when does controlled latent prediction identify both the underlying state and the controlled dynamics? This is challenging under nonlinear observations and behavior policies with limited conditional action variation, where state-dependent evolution and action effects can be statistically confounded. We establish a joint identifiability theory for controlled world models with Gaussian latent states under state-dependent Gaussian behavior policies. We identify two policy-dependent conditions: spectral separation of the predictable signal governs representation identifiability, while non-degenerate conditional action variation governs transition identifiability. We prove that when both conditions hold, every global minimizer of the JEPA objective identifies the latent state and controlled transition up to an orthogonal transformation. We further derive quantitative bounds on representation and transition identifiability under approximate optimization. Finally, we construct predictor perturbations along weakly excited action directions whose counterfactual-to-on-policy error ratio is the inverse transition-identifiability margin, revealing the cost of limited action coverage. Experiments across nonlinear observation maps and behavior policies corroborate the theory and demonstrate implications for transition identifiability, counterfactual prediction, and goal-conditioned latent planning.

</details>


### [120] [Hyperball May Not Be a Free Lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22444)
*Yihao Xiao,Jialong Sun,Zitian Gao,Zeming Wei,Chutian Wang,Ran Tao,Jiaye Teng,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了Hyperball风格优化器（如MuonH和MuonWD）在大规模训练中的表现差异。通过推导角度有效学习率，发现径向更新对角度位移影响有限。主要差异源于有效步长的演化而非更新方向。实验表明，即使Hyperball优化器保持恒定角速度，仍需精心设计学习率调度才能发挥其潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyperball风格优化器（通过固定矩阵参数范数并归一化更新）在大规模训练中表现优异，但其优势来源尚不清楚。本文旨在从参数更新的角度位移出发，探究其工作机制。

Method: 推导了角度有效学习率（考虑参数-更新角度、参数范数和更新范数），将优化器更新分解为径向和切向分量，分析径向更新对一步角度位移的影响。设计启发式实验通过修改学习率调度使不同优化器的动力学相互复现。

Result: 径向分量对角度有效学习率的直接影响有限，无法解释MuonH早期收敛慢但后期超越MuonWD的现象。主要差异源于有效步长的演化，而非Hyperball带来的更新方向优势。激进的学习率衰减可加速MuonH早期训练但损害后期性能。

Conclusion: Hyperball风格优化器并非"免费午餐"——保持恒定角速度并不能消除学习率调度问题，仍需精心设计调度策略才能发挥其潜力。

Abstract: For scale-invariant deep networks, Hyperball-style optimizers have shown strong performance in large-scale training by fixing the norms of matrix-valued parameters and normalizing updates. However, the source of their advantage remains unclear. Starting from the angular displacement between consecutive parameter states, we derive an angular effective learning rate that accounts for the parameter-update angle, parameter norm, and update norm. We also show that the conventional norm-based measure is a special case under parameter-update orthogonality. We then decompose optimizer updates into radial and tangential components and analyze how radial updates affect one-step angular displacement. Under the training configurations considered, numerical results show that the radial component has only a limited direct effect on the angular effective learning rate. It therefore cannot explain why MuonH converges more slowly than MuonWD early in training but overtakes it later. To further isolate the underlying mechanism, we devise a heuristic experiment that modifies only the learning-rate schedule so that the dynamics of each optimizer reproduce those of the other. The results suggest that their main difference stems from the evolution of the effective step size rather than an intrinsically superior update direction induced by Hyperball. Our pretraining experiments further show that more aggressive learning-rate decay can accelerate MuonH early in training but may impair its later performance. Thus, maintaining a constant angular velocity does not eliminate the learning-rate-scheduling problem; careful scheduling remains essential to realizing the potential of Hyperball-style optimizers. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mangocrazz/hyperball-may-not-be-a-free-lunch.

</details>


### [121] [Phylogenetic signal in marine mammal and bird vocalizations captured by audio foundation models: the limited benefit of domain-specific pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22458)
*Víctor Rincón Yepes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探针四个预训练音频模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio、BirdNET），发现它们无需针对系统发育任务训练，其嵌入空间便能编码物种间的系统发育距离。在32种海洋哺乳动物（26种鲸类r=0.82）和20种鸟类中，通用基础模型显著优于手工MFCC特征和领域专用模型，表明预训练音频嵌入天然蕴含进化信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证大规模预训练音频模型是否在没有任何显式监督的情况下，自发学习到了与物种进化关系相关的声学表征结构，即嵌入空间的几何形状是否隐式地追踪了生命之树。

Method: 选取四种预训练音频模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio、BirdNET），在32种海洋哺乳动物（Watkins数据库，1754条录音）和20种鸟类（Jetz et al. 2012系统发育树）两个独立辐射类群上，通过Mantel检验和偏Mantel检验评估嵌入空间距离与系统发育距离的相关性，并与手工MFCC特征进行对比。

Result: 海洋哺乳动物中，CLAP和BEATs-bio的r=0.82，AST r=0.74（p<0.001），而MFCC r=0.04（p=0.338）；降维至同等维度后差距依然显著；控制主频后偏Mantel r=0.404。鸟类中，AST r=0.55，CLAP r=0.52，而领域专用模型BirdNET和BEATs-bio仅r≈0.32-0.36。

Conclusion: 预训练音频嵌入在两个独立辐射类群中均携带了强系统发育信号，且领域专用预训练并非必要条件——通用基础模型反而优于领域专用模型，表明深层进化结构隐式地编码在广泛预训练的音频表征中。

Abstract: Do learned audio embeddings encode structure that nobody told them to encode? We probe four large pretrained audio models (AST, CLAP, BEATs-bio and BirdNET) with a downstream task none of them saw during training: recovering phylogenetic distance from species vocalizations. If the geometry of the embedding space tracks the tree of life, the representation is picking up something deeper than the labels the model was optimized for.
  We run Mantel tests across two independent radiations. In 32 marine mammal species (1,754 recordings from the Watkins Marine Mammal Sound Database) the foundation models recover strong phylogenetic signal within the 26 cetaceans (CLAP r=0.82, BEATs-bio r=0.82, AST r=0.74; all p<0.001), among the highest acoustic-phylogenetic correlations reported for any taxon. Hand-crafted MFCC features (105d) find nothing (r=0.040, p=0.338). The gap survives after PCA-projecting every embedding down to 105 dimensions, so it is not an artefact of representation size. It also survives a partial Mantel test controlling for dominant frequency (partial Mantel r=0.404, keeping 97% of the variance explained), so it is not just pitch in disguise.
  We repeat the analysis on 20 bird species using the Jetz et al. (2012) phylogeny, and this time add BirdNET, a classifier trained end-to-end on around 6,000 bird species. The general-purpose foundation models recover the signal again (AST r=0.55, CLAP r=0.52). The unexpected result is that neither BirdNET nor the bioacoustic BEATs-bio beat them (r around 0.32 to 0.36). Matching the training domain to the target taxon does not, by itself, help. Pretrained audio embeddings carry evolutionary information across two independent radiations, and domain-specific pretraining is not required for it to emerge.

</details>


### [122] [Complexity Bounds and Approaches to Learning Projected Gradient Descent Solver Iterates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22467)
*Anjian Li,Ryne Beeson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对参数优化问题中数据稀缺的挑战，提出了一种k-邻域数据收集策略，通过增强收敛解数据集与中间求解器迭代数据来增加训练数据量。基于Rademacher复杂度推导了泛化界，揭示了k-邻域及相关参数的作用。方法聚焦于投影梯度下降求解的单侧箱约束二次规划，并通过两个示例说明。该方法提升了DDDAS范式的效率，最后讨论了学习求解器迭代数据的两种观点，并与GLENS方法建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数优化问题中数据稀缺导致生成模型难以训练，而传统数值求解计算成本高昂。现有方法受限于可用数据，需要在不增加额外求解器运行的情况下提升数据量。

Method: 提出k-邻域数据收集策略，将中间求解器迭代加入训练集；聚焦投影梯度下降求解的单侧箱约束二次规划；基于Rademacher复杂度推导泛化界。

Result: 推导出包含k-邻域及相关参数的泛化界；通过两个示例验证了求解器行为；方法提升了DDDAS范式中数据-模型-优化循环的效率。

Conclusion: k-邻域数据收集策略有效缓解了数据稀缺问题，提升了生成模型的训练效果；与GLENS方法的联系为数据高效的全局搜索提供了新视角。
