Abstract: Data scarcity poses a fundamental challenge in training generative models to produce initial guesses for parametric optimization problems that are otherwise numerically expensive to solve. We therefore study a $k$-neighborhood data collection strategy that augments datasets of converged solutions with intermediate solver iterates, increasing the amount of training data without additional solver runs. To understand the benefits of this approach, we derive a generalization bound based on Rademacher complexity that reveals the role of the $k$-neighborhoods and related parameters. To achieve this result, we focus on one-sided box-constrained quadratic programs solved by projected gradient descent. We illustrate the behavior of this solver on two examples. The approach proposed in this paper enables a more capable DDDAS paradigm by improving the efficiency of the data-model-optimization loop. We finish by discussing two views of learning solver-iterate data and connect our analysis with GLENS, a new data-efficient global search method.

</details>


### [123] [\k{appa}-LoRA: Condition Numbers Reveal Which LoRA Matrices Worth Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22489)
*Jianghui Wang,Silong Yong,Francesco Orabona,Marco Canini,Katia P. Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出κ-LoRA方法，基于条件数（最大与最小奇异值之比）选择性地更新LoRA矩阵：条件数大的矩阵包含更多适应信息，条件数小的矩阵贡献有限。通过仅更新前50%条件数最大的矩阵，κ-LoRA在保持精度前提下，减少50%可训练参数、平均节省16.2%微调时间并降低4.5%内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA均匀更新所有矩阵，计算成本高，尤其不适合大模型和资源受限场景（边缘部署、设备端微调）。本文首次发现不同LoRA矩阵的贡献存在差异，条件数小的矩阵方向均衡但适应贡献小，条件数大的矩阵包含未充分发展的方向、驱动主要性能提升，因此需要更选择性的微调策略。

Method: 提出κ-LoRA方法：1) 计算各LoRA矩阵的条件数（最大奇异值/最小奇异值）；2) 按条件数降序排列，仅对前50%条件数最大的矩阵执行LoRA更新；3) 条件数小的矩阵保持冻结，从而减半可训练参数。

Result: 实验表明：κ-LoRA平均缩短16.2%微调时间，减少4.5%内存开销，精度与标准LoRA持平。此外，所选矩阵的条件数在训练过程中持续下降，说明方法有效性源于针对性的谱再平衡而非单纯参数选择。

Conclusion: 选择性更新条件数大的LoRA矩阵能在保持性能的同时显著降低计算和内存成本。条件数的下降趋势揭示了κ-LoRA通过谱再平衡机制发挥作用，为高效微调提供了新思路。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely adopted technique for efficient neural network fine-tuning, decomposing model updates into low-rank matrices. However, LoRA remains computationally costly because it updates all matrices uniformly, regardless of their actual contribution to adaptation. This cost is especially prohibitive for large-scale models with billions of parameters and for resource-constrained settings such as edge deployment and on-device fine-tuning. We show for the first time that not all LoRA matrices are equally worth tuning: matrices with smaller condition numbers (the ratio of largest to smallest singular value) are already well-balanced across directions and contribute only marginally to adaptation, whereas matrices with larger condition numbers contain underdeveloped directions that span richer subspaces and drive most of the performance gains. This observation itself is a key contribution of our work, and it motivates a more selective approach to fine-tuning. Building on this insight, we propose \k{appa}-LoRA, a method that optimizes LoRA by focusing updates on the matrices with the largest condition numbers, which capture the most informative directions of change. By restricting LoRA updates to the top 50% of weight matrices ranked by condition number, \k{appa}-LoRA halves the trainable parameter count and correspondingly reduces compute and memory cost. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that this design cuts fine-tuning time by 16.2% on average while matching the accuracy of standard LoRA and reducing memory cost by 4.5%. Further analysis reveals that the condition numbers of the selected matrices consistently decrease over training, suggesting that \k{appa}-LoRA's effectiveness stems from targeted spectral rebalancing rather than parameter selection alone.

</details>


### [124] [Susceptible Reservoir Architectures for Regime-Conditional Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22491)
*Aliaksei Kaliutau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于储层计算原理的Susceptible Architectures (SUSA)框架用于波动率预测，包含复值开链/周期储层和基于状态的专家混合模型，在美股和ETF数据上以GARCH为基准，实现了有统计显著的QLIKE改进，并与HARQ集成后进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动率预测中，持久性和测量噪声主导了信号，留给非线性模型可挖掘的残差结构非常有限。作者希望设计一种能有效提取残差中微弱非线性结构的储层架构。

Method: 提出SUSA框架：1) 复值开链储层和周期储层作为特征提取器；2) 基于四种市场状态（平静、爆发、恢复、持续压力）的门控专家混合模型解释储层特征；3) 引入开放系统q-qubit量子版本（Qiskit实现）；4) 以AR-Ridge为锚点，使用QLIKE损失的有界残差校正。训练采用三个不重叠的时间段划分（训练/验证/测试），12步输入窗口，5步预测 horizon。

Result: 在16只美股和ETF上，SUSA与GARCH表现相当，在IWM和XLP上取得统计显著的QLIKE改进；与HARQ集成后，堆叠集成相比最强基模型平均QLIKE提升0.0116，在75%的测试场景中获胜。

Conclusion: SUSA框架能有效捕捉波动率预测中的残差结构，与经典模型互补，集成策略可显著提升预测性能。

Abstract: Volatility forecasting is dominated by persistence and measurement noise, leaving limited residual structure for nonlinear models to exploit. We introduce Susceptible Architectures (SUSA), a reservoir-design principle for volatility forecasting, and its two concrete implementations, based on complex-valued open-chain and periodic reservoirs and regime-conditioned experts to interpret reservoir features across calm, onset, recovery, and persistent-stress states. We also implement open-system $q$-qubit counterparts in Qiskit while retaining a common AR-Ridge anchor and a bounded residual correction trained under QLIKE. We evaluate models on 16 U.S. equity and exchange-traded-fund series using three disjoint chronological training, validation, and test folds, a 12-observation input window, and a five-observation forecast horizon. The proposed models perform competitively with GARCH, achieving statistically significant QLIKE improvements for specific assets (IWM, XLP). Also models' forecasts complement HARQ-style predictions: a stacked ensemble improves mean QLIKE by 0.0116 over its strongest constituent and wins in 75% of test scenarios.

</details>


### [125] [Interpretable EEG biomarkers with bag-of-waves: Spatial and temporal waveform dictionaries for low-data regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22508)
*Athanasios Papastathopoulos-Katsaros,Steven T. Lee,Lin Yao,Ajay Thomas,Junseok Park,Matthew J. McGinley,Zhandong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出bag-of-waves框架，通过无监督移位不变k-means学习EEG波形模板（原子），将连续EEG转为原子令牌序列并进行计数，结合n-gram捕获时间结构及多通道空间原子。在三个数据集上（低数据小鼠聚类、痴呆分类、TUEV事件分类）与深度/基础模型性能相当，但参数极少且完全可解释，尤其适用于低数据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG分析方法存在两难：预定义谱特征存在强偏倚，提前固定了何为信息量；深度神经网络和基础模型虽强大但难以解释且需要大量数据和算力。因此亟需一种既可解释又能在低数据场景下高效工作的EEG表示学习方法。

Method: 提出bag-of-waves框架：1) 使用移位不变k-means（无监督）从EEG中学习小型字典的可解释波形模板（原子）；2) 将连续EEG转为原子令牌序列，计数后输入简单下游分类器/聚类；3) 扩展一：引入原子间转移（n-gram）捕获时间结构；4) 扩展二：从单通道原子推广到区域性和跨通道空间原子以处理多通道情况。

Result: 在三个互补数据集上：单通道小鼠基因型聚类（仅16只动物，低数据）、静息态痴呆分类（空间任务）、TUEV六分类临床EEG事件基准（高数据对比），bag-of-waves均达到与SOTA深度/基础模型竞争的性能，而参数量仅为极小部分。

Conclusion: bag-of-waves的核心优势在于：完全可解释（每个原子对应可检视波形，可恢复已知临床形态学特征供神经生理学家直接验证），参数极少，且在深度模型不适用的低数据场景下表现优异。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is widely used to diagnose neurological conditions, but its analysis usually relies on either predefined spectral features or deep neural networks. Predefined features carry a strong bias, since they fix in advance what counts as informative, while deep neural networks and foundation models are hard to interpret and need large amounts of data and compute. We present bag-of-waves, an interpretable framework that learns a small dictionary of recurring EEG waveform templates, called atoms, using shift-invariant k-means without labels. The continuous EEG is then turned into a sequence of atom tokens, whose counts feed a simple downstream classifier or clustering step. We extend this representation in two ways: we add atom-to-atom transitions, which we call n- grams, to capture temporal structure, and we move from single-channel atoms to regional and cross-channel spatial atoms for the multichannel case. We test the method on three complementary datasets, each probing a different aspect: single-channel mouse genotype clustering with only sixteen animals (the low-data and temporal case), resting-state dementia classification (the spatial case), and the TUEV benchmark, a six-way classification of clinical EEG events (a high-data comparison against strong deep and foundation baselines). Across all three datasets, bag-of-waves achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art deep and foundation models. Yet, it operates with a fraction of the parameter count and provides full interpretability: because every atom corresponds to an inspectable waveform, the method explicitly recovers known clinical morphologies that a neurophysiologist can directly validate. Its main advantage is that it works in the low-data regime where heavier models are a poor fit.

</details>


### [126] [Dysphagia Risk Stratification in Head and Neck Cancer via Two-Stage PRO-Clinical Stacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22514)
*Siyuan Zhao,Eric Ababio Anyimadu,Zachary G. Brumm,Yue Ma,Clifton David Fuller,Xinhua Zhang,G. Elisabeta Marai,Guadalupe Canahuate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于患者报告结局（PRO）与临床变量的两阶段 stacking 模型，无需视频荧光成像即可预测头颈癌幸存者的吞咽障碍风险，为吞咽毒性评估提供了可解释、低成本的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吞咽困难是头颈癌治疗后严重的迟发性副作用，但现有金标准评估（视频荧光成像）依赖专业设备和人员、患者负担重，难以在幸存者护理中常规使用；而患者报告结局虽低成本易获取，却缺乏明确的临床框架来指导何时应升级护理。

Method: 构建单次就诊的 PRO-临床预测框架，采用可解释的两阶段 stacking 模型，以 MDADI 各条目响应和结构化临床变量为输入，无需视频荧光成像，预测吞咽障碍风险。

Result: MDADI 各条目响应包含的预测信息超越复合或全局汇总分数；可解释性分析揭示了与吞咽障碍相关的症状模式和临床风险因素。

Conclusion: 结构化 PRO-临床整合可作为无影像依赖的实用吞咽困难风险分层工具，支持头颈癌幸存者的筛查与护理决策。

Abstract: Dysphagia is a debilitating late effect of head and neck cancer (HNC) treatment, yet timely identification of at-risk patients remains challenging in survivorship care. Definitive assessment relies on videofluoroscopic imaging, as captured by the Dynamic Imaging Grade of Swallowing Toxicity (CTCAE-DIGEST), which, while validated, requires specialized equipment, trained personnel, and significant patient burden, limiting its routine use in surveillance. Patient-reported outcomes (PROs), by contrast, are low-cost, scalable, and easily collected at any clinical encounter, making them an attractive alternative signal for identifying patients who may warrant further evaluation. However, a clear clinical framework for translating PRO responses into actionable interventions is still evolving. In particular, uncertainty remains regarding when a patient's self-reported symptom burden should prompt escalation of care.
  This study addresses this gap by formulating a single-visit PRO-clinical prediction framework and introducing a clinically interpretable two-stage stacking model to predict swallowing impairment risk using PRO responses and structured clinical variables, without requiring videofluoroscopic imaging. The proposed framework quantifies the independent contributions of patient-reported symptoms and clinical factors within a unified and interpretable risk assessment model. Our findings demonstrate that individual MDADI responses contain predictive information beyond that captured by composite or global summary scores, while interpretability analyses reveal symptom patterns and clinical risk factors associated with swallowing impairment. Together, these results support the use of structured PRO-clinical integration as a practical, imaging-free approach for dysphagia risk stratification in HNC survivorship.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Prior laundering: learned priors with inherited, undetectable overconfidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21721)
*Ali Siahkoohi,Sina Alemohammad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文指出，在贝叶斯逆问题中使用从历史重建档案（而非真实数据）训练的学习生成先验，会导致后验不确定性在测量无信息的盲子空间中反映的是档案的信念而非数据的真实不确定性，且无法通过部署后的自洽性检验发现。这种先验实质上是将旧正则化器通过单步EM迭代的结果，可能过于自信。实验证明扩散先验和归一化流在盲子空间中都低估不确定性，建议报告测量可分辨的方向以分离数据支持的信度与管道继承的信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震成像和医学成像等不适定贝叶斯逆问题中，真实数据稀缺，研究者转而使用历史重建档案训练生成先验（"先验洗白"），但这种方法在测量无信息的区域中会引入档案的信念而非数据的真实不确定性，且无法在部署时被检测纠正，需要揭示其根本原因和影响。

Method: 理论分析表明，对档案后验关于测量取平均等价于将旧正则化器推进一个期望最大化步；通过扩散先验（图像去模糊任务）和归一化流（非线性地下水流动算子）的数值实验，对比档案训练与真实数据训练的先验在盲子空间中的覆盖表现。

Result: 档案训练的先验在测量可分辨方向上有改善，但在盲子空间中继承旧信念且过于自信；单最佳档案更差，置信区间宽度塌缩至零。扩散先验在盲子空间下覆盖真实解，归一化流在非线性地下水算子上表现相同。

Conclusion: 建议在实践中报告测量可分辨的方向，将数据支持的信度与通过管道继承的信念分离，以正确使用先验不确定性。

Abstract: Learned generative priors are increasingly used for ill-posed Bayesian inverse problems, their posterior uncertainty treated as earned from data. But training one requires truths, scarce in seismic and medical imaging, so the recourse is an archive of legacy reconstructions---prior laundering. Where the measurements are uninformative the posterior reverts to the prior, so the uncertainty reported there is the archive's, not the data's, and nothing in deployment reveals it: truths differing only on those directions induce identical data laws, and self-consistency checks such as simulation-based calibration pass whatever the prior believes. That belief has an exact source: averaging the legacy posterior over the measurements yields the old regularizer advanced a single expectation--maximization step---improved where the data resolve, frozen where they cannot. It is overconfident wherever the inherited belief is tighter than the truth. A single-best archive is worse, collapsing the blind credible interval to zero width. Deployed, a diffusion prior fit to the archive under-covers the operator's blind subspace, unlike a truth-trained control, and a normalizing flow does the same on a nonlinear groundwater operator. We recommend reporting which directions the measurements resolve, separating confidence the data support from belief inherited through the pipeline.

</details>


### [128] [Simulation-Based Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21843)
*Xinwei Shen,Diana Cai,Cheng Zhang,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了模拟驱动的经验贝叶斯（SBEB），将非参数经验贝叶斯与基于模拟的推断相结合，在似然函数仅可通过模拟器获取的情况下实现经验贝叶斯估计，并通过迭代优化先验分布提升了推断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典经验贝叶斯要求似然函数 p(x|z) 可解析计算，但许多科学应用中的似然函数仅能通过模拟器获取，无法直接应用经验贝叶斯方法。

Method: 提出SBEB方法，利用观测数据、模拟器样本和摊销推断网络，在无需显式密度函数的情况下计算经验贝叶斯估计，并迭代地将拟合的经验贝叶斯先验向总体先验优化。

Result: 在多个科学模拟器和真实数据集上的实验表明，SBEB相比使用固定先验的基于模拟的推断方法，显著提升了推断精度。

Conclusion: SBEB成功将经验贝叶斯扩展到隐式似然场景，为仅能通过模拟器获取似然的科学应用提供了一种有效的推断框架。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) performs simultaneous inference across many related latent variables. Classical EB assumes that the likelihood p(x | z) is tractable. In many scientific applications, however, the likelihood is available only through a simulator. This paper develops EB for such implicit likelihoods. We introduce simulation-based empirical Bayes (SBEB), which connects nonparametric EB to simulation-based inference (SBI). SBEB computes EB estimates without an explicit density by using the observed data, simulator samples, and an amortized inference network. SBEB iteratively refines the fitted EB prior toward the population prior. With several scientific simulators and real-world data, we demonstrate that SBEB improves accuracy over SBI with a fixed prior.

</details>


### [129] [Efficient Online LLM Watermark Detection via Rao-Blackwellized E-Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21958)
*Lu Luo,Dandan Mo,Chengdong Xu,Ting Li,Jinhan Xie,Huiqiong Li,Niansheng Tang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Rao-Blackwellized e-过程的在线水印检测框架，支持任意有效停止的流式文本检测，并在 Gumbel-max 水印上实现了理论保证与高效实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型水印检测方法多为固定视界程序，无法在流式生成中实现有效的早期停止，缺乏在线检测与任意有效推断能力。

Method: 基于 Rao-Blackwellized e-过程构建在线水印检测框架，支持递归式 token 级证据更新；将 Gumbel-max 水印的 token 级依赖检验转化为 pivot 诱导的序贯检验问题，并推导显式零分布。

Result: 理论上证明了任意有效停止下第一类错误控制以及水印下的正向对数增长一致性；仿真与真实 LLM 文本实验验证了高效的在线检测性能。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了流式文本生成中具有严格任意有效保证的高效在线水印检测，为 LLM 文本的可靠区分提供了新方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed, reliable and efficient mechanisms for distinguishing AI-generated text from human-written content have become essential. Statistical watermarking has emerged as a promising solution, yet most existing methods are typically fixed-horizon procedures, precluding valid early stopping in streaming generation. In this paper, we develop an efficient online watermark detection framework with anytime-valid inference based on Rao-Blackwellized e-processes, enabling recursive token-level evidence updates without storing the full history. In particular, we instantiate the framework for the Gumbel-max watermark and reduce the original token-level dependence testing problem to a pivot-induced sequential testing problem with an explicit null distribution. Theoretically, we prove anytime-valid Type I error control under arbitrary optional stopping and establish positive asymptotic log-growth under watermarking, implying consistency of the proposed stopping rules. Simulations and experiments on real LLM-generated text demonstrate efficient online detection with rigorous anytime-valid guarantees.

</details>


### [130] [Convergence analysis of a family of Zermelo-type iterations for the Bradley--Terry model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22221)
*Ruijian Han,Ding Lu,Yiming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对Bradley-Terry模型中Zermelo型不动点迭代的参数化族（α=0为加速变体）进行了局部收敛性理论分析，证明了异步更新下α=0是最优选择，且加速主要源于异步更新机制而非参数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Newman提出的α参数化Zermelo算法族中，α=0经验上收敛更快但机制不明，本文旨在通过局部收敛分析提供理论解释。

Method: 推导同步和异步更新下局部收敛因子的闭式表达式，通过雅可比矩阵谱分析研究收敛因子对α的依赖关系；在总体BT模型下建立渐近逼近结果。

Result: 同步更新下α<1可能发散，局部收敛因子关于α拟凸；异步更新下始终局部收敛，且收敛因子关于α单调递增，从而α=0最优；数值实验验证理论。

Conclusion: α=0的加速主要归因于异步更新策略而非参数选择本身，为实际应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Zermelo's algorithm is a classical method for computing the maximum likelihood estimator in the Bradley--Terry (BT) model, but its convergence can be slow in practice. To accelerate computation, Newman introduced a family of Zermelo-type fixed-point iterations parameterized by $α$, with Zermelo's algorithm recovered at $α=1$. Empirical evidence suggests that the choice $α=0$ often converges substantially faster, making it a promising alternative, yet the mechanism underlying this acceleration remains elusive. This paper provides theoretical insight into this phenomenon through a systematic local convergence analysis. We derive closed-form expressions for local convergence factors under synchronous and asynchronous updates and analyze their dependence on $α$ via spectral analysis of the associated Jacobian matrices. For synchronous updates, we show that the algorithm may fail to converge when $α<1$, and its local convergence factor is quasi-convex in $α$ under the population BT model. In contrast, asynchronous updates are always locally convergent, and their local convergence factor is provably monotonically increasing in $α$ under the population BT model of consistently ordered bipartite comparison graphs, establishing the optimality of $α=0$ in this setting. We further establish asymptotic approximation results for the population convergence factors under the BT model, justifying their practical relevance. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm the theory. Our analysis complements existing convergence results and shows that the acceleration of $α=0$ arises not only from the parameter choice but, more importantly, from the use of asynchronous updates.

</details>


### [131] [Variational Low-rank Tensor Decomposition for Multisubject Spatiotemporal Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22262)
*Laura M. Montaldo,Ricardo A. Borsoi,Sebastian Miron,Tulay Adali*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空变分张量分解（ST-VTD）框架，结合张量分解生成模型与结构化先验，联合表示多被试fMRI数据中的空间图谱和时间动态，通过LL1分解启发的低秩空间正则化、基于LSTM的自适应时间先验以及展开优化算法的摊销变分推理，在合成fMRI数据上显著优于传统分解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多被试时空数据（尤其是神经影像数据）中，共享和被试特异结构的建模面临挑战，现有矩阵和张量分解依赖固定的多线性结构或耦合机制，灵活性和复杂变异性捕捉能力受限。

Method: 提出ST-VTD框架：（1）采用张量分解生成模型，空间因子受LL1分解启发的低秩正则化约束；（2）时间因子通过基于LSTM的学得性先验建模，实现灵活自适应动力学；（3）利用展开优化算法的摊销变分推断进行后验推理，构建可解释且参数高效的架构；（4）采用基于组独立成分分析的热启动策略提升优化性能。

Result: 在逼真的合成fMRI数据集上，该方法相较于代表性经典和概率分解基准，显著提升了潜在因子的恢复性能。

Conclusion: ST-VTD框架通过结构化先验与摊销变分推理的结合，有效解决了多被试时空数据中共享与特异结构的联合建模问题，为神经影像数据分析提供了更灵活且可解释的工具。

Abstract: Modeling shared and subject-specific structure in multisubject spatiotemporal data remains challenging, particularly in neuroimaging, where both spatial and temporal patterns exhibit rich variability across subjects. Existing matrix and tensor decompositions provide interpretable factorizations, but rely on fixed multilinear structures or coupling schemes that may limit their flexibility in capturing complex variability. In this work, we introduce a spatiotemporal variational tensor decomposition (ST-VTD) framework that combines a tensor factorization generative model with structured priors to jointly represent spatial maps and temporal dynamics. Spatial factors are regularized to promote a low-rank structure inspired by the LL1 decomposition, while temporal factors are modeled using a learned Long short-term memory (LSTM)-based prior, enabling flexible and adaptive dynamics. Posterior inference is performed using an amortized variational formulation by unrolling iterations of an optimization algorithm, leading to an interpretable and parameter-efficient architecture. The proposed inference framework employs a warm-start strategy based on group independent component analysis, which we found to improve optimization performance. Experiments on a realistic synthetic functional MRI (fMRI) dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves latent factor recovery compared with representative classical and probabilistic decomposition benchmarks.

</details>


### [132] [General Value Functions for Remaining Useful Life and Failure-Mode Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22268)
*Hao Yan,Ali Sarabi,Qing Zou,Boyang Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将剩余使用寿命预测和故障模式分类统一建模为向量广义价值函数预测问题，采用多步时序差分估计器TD(n,λ)替代传统监督学习方法，在标签稀缺和身份信息缺失的场景下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定窗口监督学习无法自然编码退化状态预测之间的时序递归关系，且需要完整的终端标签和单元身份信息，限制了碎片化、身份缺失的退化数据的利用。

Method: 将预测性维护中的RUL预测和故障模式分类形式化为吸收退化过程上的向量广义价值函数预测，使用多步时序差分估计器TD(n,λ)进行估计；从理论上识别了向量GVF的Bellman不动点，刻画了线性投影TD极限及其与完全返回蒙特卡洛回归的关系。

Result: 在事件触发多模仿真和NASA C-MAPSS标签稀缺拼接数据上，TD方法在RUL和故障模式预测上均优于相同骨干网络的监督式蒙特卡洛控制，尤其在完整标签稀缺时优势显著。

Conclusion: 碎片化、无身份标识的退化记录可以通过贡献局部Bellman转移来参与训练，而无需被丢弃直到获得完整运行至故障标签，提高了数据利用效率。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and failure-mode classification are central tasks in predictive maintenance. Many data-driven pipelines use fixed-window supervised learning with complete terminal labels; such routes do not naturally encode the temporal recursion linking successive degradation-state predictions when observations are partial or unit identities are unavailable. We formulate prognostics as vector General Value Function (GVF) prediction on an absorbing degradation process, treating RUL and failure-mode probabilities as temporally consistent targets rather than independent window-level labels, and estimate them with a multi-step temporal-difference estimator, TD($n,λ$). Supporting theory identifies the Bellman fixed point of the vector GVFs, characterizes the linear projected-TD limit and its relation to complete-return Monte Carlo regression under realizability, and explains when bootstrapped TD targets are less variable than Monte Carlo returns. On an event-triggered multimode simulation and NASA C-MAPSS label-scarce stitch data, TD improves RUL and failure-mode prediction relative to a supervised same-backbone Monte Carlo control, especially under scarce complete labels. Practically, fragmented, identity-free degradation records can contribute local Bellman transitions instead of being discarded until complete run-to-failure labels are available.

</details>


### [133] [Hopformer: Homogeneity-Pursuit Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22299)
*Wan Zhang,Qinjie Lin,Chan Lee,Weijian Li,Han Liu,Kai Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Hopformer是一个两阶段框架，结合SPA同质化层和LoRA微调Transformer，用于处理高维协变量下的多时间序列预测，在多个基准上平均MASE提升6.56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多时间序列预测中统一公共时间模式同时保留序列特异性信息的核心挑战。

Method: 第一阶段使用Sparsity Pattern Aggregation (SPA)提取包含协变量的公共低方差趋势作为同质化层；第二阶段用LoRA微调Transformer对残差中的复杂依赖关系建模。

Result: 在合成和真实世界预测基准上，平均MASE提升6.56%，达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: Hopformer通过理论保证（SPA的oracle不等式实现近最优偏差-方差权衡，以及第二阶段在依赖时间序列数据下的泛化界）验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Forecasting multiple time-series with high-dimensional covariates presents a core challenge: unifying common temporal patterns while retaining meaningful series-specific information. We introduce Hopformer (Homogeneity-Pursuit Transformer), a two-stage framework that addresses this challenge. In the first stage, we perform a Sparsity Pattern Aggregation (SPA) scheme extracting a common low-variance trend that incorporates the covariates. This acts as a homogenization layer. In the second stage, a LoRA-fine-tuned Transformer models the remaining complex dependencies in the residual. Our method is theoretically grounded. We prove that SPA achieves a near-optimal bias-variance trade-off via an oracle inequality. We also provide generalization bounds for the second stage under dependent time series data. Hopformer sets a new state of the art, improving MASE by an average of 6.56% across synthetic and real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [134] [Learning Bidirectional Causal Interactions with Heteroscedastic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22313)
*Masahiro Tanaka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SEM-DNN，一种异方差神经联立方程估计器，利用条件协方差对角化实现无外部工具变量的双向结构效应识别，并证明其在非线性高维设定下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中估计同期双向因果关系十分困难，因为每个结果变量都内生依赖于另一个，而灵活回归只能捕捉简化式依赖关系，现有方法常需要外部工具变量。

Method: 提出SEM-DNN方法，利用条件协方差对角化策略：当结构冲击条件均值为零、给定协变量条件不相关且条件方差非比例时，只有真实交互系数能在整个特征空间对角化条件残差协方差。方法联合逼近非线性结构均值函数和特征依赖方差，使用包含联立系统雅可比的对角高斯拟似然。建立唯一识别和正定局部曲率，提出神经-剖面兼容条件以解决网络参数化非唯一性问题。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验表明，在非线性、高维 nuisance 函数和非高斯冲击设定下，SEM-DNN随着信息增加比参数化、核方法和分别估计的神经网络替代方案更可靠地恢复结构效应，尽管计算成本更高。即食谷物扫描数据应用展示了该方法在研究同期价格-销售反馈中的应用。

Conclusion: SEM-DNN为无工具变量的同期双向因果关系识别提供了一种可行且有效的估计方法，在非线性高维场景下性能优越，计算成本是主要限制。

Abstract: Estimating contemporaneous bidirectional interactions from observational data is difficult because each outcome is endogenous to the other, while flexible regressions may capture only reduced-form dependence. This paper proposes SEM-DNN, a heteroscedastic neural simultaneous-equation estimator that learns reciprocal structural interactions without external instruments. Identification exploits conditional covariance diagonalization: when structural shocks have zero conditional means, are conditionally uncorrelated given predetermined covariates, and exhibit nonproportional conditional variances, only the true interaction coefficients diagonalize the conditional residual covariance across the feature space. The method jointly approximates nonlinear structural mean functions and feature-dependent variances using a diagonal Gaussian quasi-likelihood that incorporates the simultaneous-system Jacobian. We establish unique identification and positive-definite local curvature of the profiled population criterion and show that, under neural-profile compatibility conditions, the implemented neural criterion inherits this curvature despite nonunique network parameterizations. The coefficients admit a causal interpretation when the structural equations represent autonomous mechanisms that remain invariant under the relevant interventions. Monte Carlo experiments with nonlinear, high-dimensional nuisance functions and non-Gaussian shocks show that SEM-DNN recovers structural effects more reliably than parametric, kernel-based, and separate-equation neural alternatives as information increases, although at greater computational cost. An application to ready-to-eat cereal scanner data illustrates how the method can study contemporaneous price-sales feedback and assess identification strength, residual diagonalization, variance calibration, and optimization sensitivity.

</details>


### [135] [Learning Ergodic Dynamical Systems from a Finite Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22399)
*Oleksii Kachaiev,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了遍历随机动力系统单条有限轨迹的学习问题，提出了一种基于非线性最小二乘的最优一步预测函数估计方法，并给出了关于过程不变测度的高概率保证。研究将框架扩展到高阶系统和有限状态空间，并展示了相同的最小二乘和浓度论证可自然扩展到学习Koopman算子。方法结合了统计学习理论和马尔可夫链定量遍历理论，核心依赖于一致几何遍历马尔可夫链的Hilbert空间值加性泛函的浓度不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统计学习理论主要针对独立同分布数据，而实际中许多系统（如动力系统）产生的轨迹数据具有非独立、非同分布的特性。已有工作对独立同分布设定下的学习问题研究充分，但对单个轨迹（非独立非同分布）的统计学习保障尚不明确，特别是在遍历动力系统框架下。本文旨在弥合该差距，为从单条轨迹中进行统计学习提供理论基础。

Method: 1) 将问题建模为离散时间自治随机系统的时间齐次马尔可夫过程；2) 采用非线性最小二乘估计最优一步预测函数；3) 利用一致几何遍历马尔可夫链的Hilbert空间值加性泛函的浓度不等式推导高概率保证；4) 将框架推广至高阶系统和有限状态空间；5) 将相同的最小二乘与浓度论证应用于Koopman算子的学习。

Result: 针对遍历动力系统的单条轨迹学习问题，建立了关于过程不变测度的高概率保证，明确了轨迹数据的非独立非同分布性质如何改变经典统计学习分析。结果同时覆盖了高阶系统、有限状态空间以及Koopman算子学习等扩展场景。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在遍历动力系统单条轨迹上进行统计学习是可行的，且所提出的最小二乘框架和浓度论证具有通用性，能够推广到多种设定（高阶系统、有限状态空间、Koopman算子学习）。方法的核心是统计学习理论与马尔可夫链定量遍历理论的有机结合。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning from a single finite trajectory of an ergodic stochastic dynamical system. More precisely, we study discrete-time autonomous stochastic systems defining time-homogeneous Markov processes. We first focus on estimating the optimal one-step prediction function by nonlinear least squares, and derive high-probability guarantees measured with respect to the invariant measure of the process. These results make explicit how the non-independent and non-identically distributed nature of trajectory data modifies the classical statistical learning analysis. We then extend the framework to higher-order systems and finite-state spaces. Finally, we show that the same least squares and concentration arguments naturally extend to learning Koopman operators. Our approach combines tools from statistical learning theory and quantitative ergodic theory for Markov chains. It relies, in particular, on a concentration inequality for Hilbert-space-valued additive functionals of uniformly geometrically ergodic Markov chains.

</details>


### [136] [Graph-Based Correlation Matrix Generation: A Convex Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22436)
*Ali Fakhar,K{é}vin Polisano,Ir{è}ne Gannaz,Sophie Achard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于凸优化的框架，用于生成具有指定图结构稀疏模式的理论相关矩阵，通过将初始矩阵投影到椭球面上并施加半正定约束，同时允许控制非对角元素分布的均值，为图模型推断的统计方法基准测试提供了灵活且可调控的生成工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生成具有图结构约束的相关矩阵时灵活性不足，无法对非对角元素分布的均值进行控制，导致生成矩阵难以反映真实数据的统计特性。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一种可调控的生成方法以服务于图模型推断方法的基准测试。

Method: 提出凸优化框架：将初始矩阵投影到椭球面（elliptope）上，施加半正定性约束，非对角元素中对应图中缺失边的位置固定为零，对角线固定为一。实现了多种数值方案并进行比较。理论上证明了在一般设置和额外均值约束下解的存在性。

Result: 仿真实验展示了生成矩阵相对于图结构的统计特性；将该方法应用于神经科学和金融两个真实数据集，并与基于GAN的相关矩阵生成方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该方法并非旨在生成均匀分布的可行集，而是提供了一种有理论保证的、可调控的方式来构建适合图模型推断基准测试的相关矩阵，在灵活性上优于现有方法。

Abstract: This work addresses the generation of theoretical correlation matrices with prescribed sparsity patterns associated to graph structures. We propose a novel convex optimization framework in which an initial matrix is projected onto an elliptope under a positive semidefiniteness constraint. Several numerical schemes are implemented and compared. The problem falls within the broader class of matrix completion, where off-diagonal entries corresponding to absent edges are fixed to zero and diagonal entries are fixed to one. Beyond this structural constraint, the approach offers greater flexibility than existing methods by allowing control over the mean of the off-diagonal entry distribution, enabling the generation of correlation matrices that better reflect realistic data. This procedure is not designed to yield a uniform distribution over the feasible set; rather, it provides a principled and tunable way to construct correlation matrices suitable for benchmarking statistical methods for graphical model inference. Theoretical guarantees on the existence of solutions are established, both in the general setting and under the additional mean constraint. Simulation studies illustrate the properties of the generated matrices with respect to graph structure. The methodology is applied to two real-world datasets from neuroscience and finance, and a comparison with GAN-based correlation matrix generation is provided.

</details>


### [137] [CausalForge: A Formally Grounded, Self-Improving Agentic Framework for Automated Research in Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22511)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CausalForge 是一个基于 Lean 证明助手的因果推断自动化理论研究框架，结合形式化验证库 Causalean 和自改进智能体管道 CausalSmith，通过机器验证的证明和语句审计来确保研究成果的可靠性，解决了 LLM 评审者不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评审者在评估自动化理论研究结果时不可靠，对伪造论文的检测率接近随机水平，因此需要一种更可靠的形式化验证方法来自动化因果推断的理论研究。

Method: 构建因果推断的 Lean 形式化库 Causalean（含 7,035 个机器检查的声明），结合自改进智能体管道 CausalSmith，该管道负责选择研究主题、提出结果、形式化陈述、构造证明，并在内核验证基础上增加语句审计环节，比对形式化定理与非正式声明的一致性。

Result: 完成了自主研究运行的评估，生成了可供人类检查的研究成果，源码、形式化库和运行记录均已开源。

Conclusion: 机器验证的证明只能保证形式化陈述从假设中推导得出，不能保证其忠实反映预期的科学声明，因此需要语句审计来弥合形式化验证与科学有效性之间的差距。

Abstract: Automating theoretical research is constrained not only by the generation of candidate results, but also by their reliable evaluation. A common approach is to close the research loop with a large language model (LLM) reviewer. However, such reviewers remain empirically unreliable: they may accept fabricated papers and detect them at rates close to chance (Bad Scientist, 2025). We present CausalForge, a framework for automated theoretical research in causal inference grounded in the Lean proof assistant. CausalForge combines Causalean, a foundational Lean library for causal inference containing 7,035 machine-checked declarations developed with language-model assistance under human design and review, with CausalSmith, a self-improving agentic pipeline that selects research topics, proposes results, formalizes statements, constructs proofs, and presents the resulting artifacts for human inspection. Because a machine-checked proof establishes only that a formal statement follows from its assumptions, not that the statement faithfully captures the intended scientific claim, the pipeline augments kernel verification with a statement audit that compares each formal theorem against the informal claim it is intended to express. We evaluate the system using artifacts produced by completed autonomous research runs. The source code, formal library, and run records are available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/CausalForge.

</details>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Safe Stabilizing Linear Feedback: Necessary and Sufficient Conditions, Optimality, and Margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21794)
*Pol Mestres,Shima Sadat Mousavi,Pio Ong,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性系统与仿射安全约束，设计了同时满足高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）约束和全局指数稳定性的线性反馈控制器。首先刻画了所有全局满足HOCBF约束的线性增益矩阵的精确类，给出了该类非空的充要条件；随后利用CBF输出动力学与CBF内部动力学概念，给出了该类矩阵中存在镇定增益的充要条件；最后展示了LQR和鲁棒控制问题可在该类安全稳定增益矩阵框架下通过代数Riccati方程和线性矩阵不等式等标准线性控制技术求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制屏障函数（CBF）虽广泛应用于安全约束下的自主系统控制设计，但已有工作表明CBF控制器可能导致系统内部动力学失稳。本文旨在解决线性系统中安全约束与稳定性之间的矛盾，即设计既能满足高阶CBF安全约束又能保证全局指数稳定性的线性反馈控制器。

Method: 本文针对线性系统与仿射安全约束，首先完整刻画了所有全局满足HOCBF约束的线性增益矩阵集合，并推导了该类非空的充要条件；然后借助CBF输出动力学与CBF内部动力学的概念，给出了在该类矩阵中存在能镇定系统的增益矩阵的充要条件；最后将LQR和鲁棒控制问题约束在该安全稳定增益矩阵类中，通过代数Riccati方程和线性矩阵不等式等标准线性控制技术求解。

Result: 成功推导出全局满足HOCBF约束的线性增益矩阵的完整类及其非空的充要条件；给出了该类中存在镇定增益矩阵的充要条件；证明了LQR和鲁棒控制问题可在该框架下通过标准线性控制技术求解，并通过仿真验证了结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为线性系统在安全约束下实现全局指数稳定的控制器设计提供了充要条件的理论框架，并将安全性与稳定性约束统一到标准线性控制设计方法中，为CBF理论与传统线性最优/鲁棒控制的结合开辟了路径。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) have become an important controller design tool for autonomous systems subject to safety constraints. Despite their popularity, recent works have shown that CBF-based controllers can destabilize the internal dynamics of the system. In this paper, we consider linear systems with affine safety constraints and design linear feedback controllers that satisfy high-order CBF (HOCBF) constraints while rendering the origin globally exponentially stable. We first characterize the exact class of all linear gain matrices that globally satisfy the HOCBF constraints, including necessary and sufficient conditions for when this class is nonempty. Then, by leveraging the recently introduced notion of CBF output dynamics and CBF internal dynamics, we provide the necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of stabilizing gain matrices within that class. Finally, we show that Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) and robust control problems can be solved while being constrained within this class of safe and stabilizing gain matrices, through standard linear control techniques such as algebraic Riccati equations (AREs) and Linear Matrix Inequalities (LMIs). We illustrate our results in a simulation example.

</details>


### [139] [On convergence of solutions to nonlocal optimal control problems with quasi-minimization constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21822)
*Joshua M. Siktar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类新型非局部最优控制问题，其中约束条件涉及拟凸能量最小化子的近似。通过与依赖于非局部梯度的能量密度相关的拟凸能量，寻找拟最小化子作为约束，解决了以往工作中因能量最小化子不唯一而导致的收敛性难题，证明了当水平参数和分数参数趋于极限时，非局部最优控制问题的解收敛到局部PDE约束最优控制问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cueto-Siktar 2026工作中，将约束条件设为全局最小化子时，由于能量可能不具有唯一最小化子，无法证明非局部最优控制问题的解收敛到相应的局部PDE约束最优控制问题的解。本研究旨在通过改用拟最小化子作为约束条件，克服这一局限性，获得更强的收敛结果。

Method: 将最优控制问题的约束条件从寻找全局最小化子改为寻找关于拟凸能量的拟最小化子。问题由分数参数s∈(0,1)和水平参数δ>0参数化，能量密度依赖于非局部分数梯度。通过这一新的约束选择，建立解的存在性，并证明当参数趋近极限时解向局部PDE约束问题的收敛性。

Result: 证明了尽管感兴趣的拟凸能量可能不具有唯一最小化子，但该类控制问题仍然存在解。更重要的是，获得了更强的收敛结果：对于具有依赖于非局部梯度的一般成本泛函的非局部最优控制问题，其最小化子收敛到对应的局部PDE约束最优控制问题的解。

Conclusion: 通过将约束条件从全局最小化子改为拟最小化子，成功解决了非局部最优控制问题向局部PDE约束问题收敛的难题。这一方法有效应对了能量最小化子不唯一带来的困难，为非局部到局部极限过渡提供了理论保证。

Abstract: This paper studies a new class of nonlocal optimal control problems where the constraints involve approximations of minimizers of quasiconvex energies. These problems are parameterized by a fractional parameter $s \in (0, 1)$ and a horizon parameter $δ> 0$, and the energy density depends on a nonlocal fractional gradient. Here, the constraint consists of finding quasi-minimizers with respect to the quasiconvex energy. Despite the fact that the energies of interest may not have unique minimizers, we may prove the existence of solutions to this class of control problems.
  The constraint in Cueto-Siktar 2026 was finding global minimizers of the energy, and this problem's main limitation was an inability to prove convergence of solutions for the nonlocal control problems to those of a corresponding local, PDE-constrained optimal control problem. While this issue arises from the lack of uniqueness of minimizers for the constraining energy, we get stronger convergence results with our new choice of constraints. Namely, we obtain convergence of minimizers for nonlocal optimal control problems that have a general cost functional depending on the nonlocal gradient.

</details>


### [140] [Stackelberg Games with a Robust Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21890)
*Juan Li,Jianfeng Zhang,Zimu Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个领导者与多个追随者构成的Stackelberg博弈。领导者选择策略后，追随者之间进行纳什博弈（可能存在多个均衡）。稳健领导者考虑最坏情况，即追随者选择对领导者最不利的均衡。通过弱形式化，该问题转化为一个时间不一致的开环零和博弈问题，并通过Wasserstein空间上的HJB方程来刻画。委托-代理问题是该问题的一种带约束特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Stackelberg博弈中，当多个追随者的纳什博弈存在多重均衡时，领导者面临不确定性。现有文献通常假设追随者会选择对领导者有利或特定的均衡，但现实中领导者可能面临追随者选择最不利均衡的风险。本文旨在为这种"稳健领导者"场景提供理论框架和数学刻画。

Method: 采用弱形式化方法，将原问题转化为开环零和博弈问题；利用Wasserstein空间上概率测度的HJB方程进行刻画。

Result: 成功将具有多重均衡的稳健Stackelberg博弈转化为时间不一致的开环零和博弈，并通过Wasserstein空间上的HJB方程给出了该问题的表征。同时指出委托-代理问题可作为带约束的特例纳入该框架。

Conclusion: 建立了处理多重均衡下稳健Stackelberg博弈的理论框架，为更广泛的委托-代理问题提供了统一的数学工具。

Abstract: In this paper we study a Stackelberg game with one leader and multiple followers. Given the leader's control, the followers solve a Nash game with possibly multiple equilibria. We consider a robust leader who considers the worst scenario, namely the followers would select the equilibrium worst for the leader. By using the weak formulation, the problem induces a zero sum game problem with open loop controls, which is time inconsistent. We shall characterize the last problem through an HJB equation on the Wasserstein space of probability measures. The principal-agent problem with one principal and multiple agents can be viewed as a special case of our problem, but with certain constraints.

</details>


### [141] [Strategic Inference of Adversarial Navigation Objectives for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21945)
*Ruimeng Hu,Xu Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性水下无人航行场景中的目的地推断问题。红方航行器在空间变化的流场中沿HJ时间最优路径前往未知目的地，蓝方观测含噪轨迹。作者推导了连续时间似然模型，给出了闭式局部MLE、常记忆多时段估计器及渐近CRLB。Fisher信息由策略均值灵敏度、参考路径灵敏度和乘性漂移灵敏度三个加性分量构成，三者均通过Jacobi场与流场几何相关联。数值实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性水下无人航行场景中，红方航行器在空间变化的流场中沿时间最优路径前往未知目的地，蓝方通过观测含噪轨迹推断目的地。现有方法缺乏对连续时间观测模型和流场几何对推断能力影响的严格理论分析。

Method: 1) 建立连续时间似然模型；2) 推导闭式局部MLE和多时段常记忆估计器；3) 进行渐近CRLB分析，Fisher信息分解为策略均值灵敏度、参考路径灵敏度和乘性漂移灵敏度三个分量，均由HJ特征流的Jacobi场诱导；4) 扩展到扫描相关观测模型和主动扫描设计问题；5) 在涡旋流和通道剪切流中进行数值验证。

Result: 获得闭式局部最大似然估计器、常记忆多时段估计器和渐近Cramér-Rao效率结果。Fisher信息由三个灵敏度分量组成，其主导几何贡献是当前流场Hessian与HJ特征流Jacobi场的收缩。数值实验验证了CRLB预测，揭示了流场几何如何决定哪些目的地可被可靠推断。

Conclusion: 建立了流场几何与目的地可识别性之间的可计算联系，证明了Jacobi场是连接三者灵敏度的核心几何量。该框架可扩展至扫描依赖观测模型和主动扫描设计，为对抗性水下导航中的推断与反推断策略提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We study destination inference for adversarial unmanned underwater navigation in a spatially varying current field. The red vehicle is modeled as approximately following a Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) time-optimal path toward an unknown destination, while a blue vehicle observes a noisy realization of that trajectory. We derive a continuous-time likelihood model for this problem and obtain a closed-form local maximum likelihood estimator, a constant-memory multi-period estimator, and an asymptotic Cramér-Rao efficiency result. The Fisher information is governed by a combined score kernel with two additive components, a policy-mean sensitivity and a reference-path sensitivity, and a multiplicative drift sensitivity whose leading geometric contribution is a contraction of the current Hessian with the Jacobi field of the HJ characteristic flow. All three sensitivities are induced by that Jacobi field, yielding a computable link between current-field geometry and destination identifiability. We further extend the framework to a sweep-dependent observation model and formulate an active sweep-design problem for the blue team. Numerical experiments in vortex and channel-shear currents validate the Cramér-Rao prediction and illustrate how current geometry determines which destinations can be reliably inferred.

</details>


### [142] [Learning from Downstream Prices: Robust Upstream Decisions with Revealed Pricing Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22027)
*Zhiqiang Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究上游企业如何利用下游零售商的最优定价决策信息（而非仅将价格视为协变量）来推断消费者估值分布，并在交易数据稀疏的情况下构建可处理的鲁棒决策规则。通过揭示最优价格蕴含的虚拟价值条件，作者提出了一种形状约束的模糊集方法，将连续分布问题简化为两场景鲁棒报童问题，显著提升了利润表现（3条记录使平均利润比从0.892提升至0.955，5条记录从0.918提升至0.986），且计算速度比传统网格方法快700倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统需求分析仅将零售价格视为协变量，忽略了价格是由更了解市场信息的零售商最优决策产生的这一事实。本文旨在利用价格的最优性信息，帮助上游企业在交易历史稀疏的条件下更好地推断消费者估值分布，从而做出更优的库存或定价决策。

Method: 在正则消费者估值假设下，利用下游零售商最优价格揭示的虚拟价值条件（virtual-value condition）和该价格处的需求分位数，构建形状约束的模糊集（shape-constrained ambiguity set），其可行局部族为标量区间。通过证明最坏情况局部模型在端点处达到，将连续分布问题简化为两场景鲁棒报童问题。同时，分析了决策误差对信息优势的侵蚀效应。

Result: 在六种正则估值模型下，3条历史记录使平均Oracle利润比从0.892提升至0.955，5条记录从0.918提升至0.986。所提出的端点方法匹配包含1001个格点的模糊网格方法的效果，但计算速度快700倍以上。

Conclusion: 下游价格应被视为战略性决策数据而非单纯的需求协变量。本文框架使上游企业能将少量定价记录转化为可处理的决策规则，同时在下游决策不可靠时提供原则性的回退方案（仅使用分位数信息）。

Abstract: Problem definition. An upstream firm often observes the retail prices and sales generated by a better-informed downstream partner, but not the consumer valuation distribution that drives those decisions. Conventional demand analysis treats a retail price as a covariate. We study how the upstream firm can instead exploit the fact that the observed price was chosen optimally, particularly when the transaction history is sparse.
  Methodology/results. Under regular consumer valuations, a downstream retailer's optimal price reveals a virtual-value condition in addition to the demand quantile observed at that price. We use these signals to construct a shape-constrained ambiguity set whose feasible local family is a scalar interval. We show that the worst-case local model is attained at an endpoint, reducing a continuous distributional problem to a two-scenario robust newsvendor. We also characterize how revealed optimality tightens a quantile-only benchmark and how bounded decision errors erode this advantage. Across six regular valuation models, three (five) historical records raise the mean oracle-profit ratio from 0.892 to 0.955 (0.918 to 0.986); the endpoint method matches a 1,001-point ambiguity grid and is more than 700 times faster.
  Managerial implications. Downstream prices should be treated as strategic decision data rather than merely as demand covariates. The proposed framework allows an upstream firm to convert a small number of pricing records into a tractable decision rule, while providing a principled fallback to quantile-only information when downstream decisions are unreliable.

</details>


### [143] [Designing Eco-Efficient Biowaste Valorisation Systems: Effects of Transport Distance and Facility Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22092)
*Christian Ottini,Gwenola Yannou-Le Bris,Sandra Domenek,Felipe Buendia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究构建了统一的技术经济和生命周期评估框架，量化分析了运输距离和处理规模对生物废物堆肥和厌氧消化系统的影响。结果显示，零距离堆肥的综合影响最低，45种配置在环境和经济上均优于当地焚烧，111种虽成本较高但在环境上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲各地生物废物单独收集的推广引发了关于环境和经济最优处理策略的问题，特别是分散式与集中式基础设施的平衡以及技术选择（堆肥与厌氧消化）对性能的影响。运输距离、设施规模、季节性废物波动和技术效率等因素之间的阈值距离和尺度依赖相互作用尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 开发统一的技术经济和生命周期评估框架，评估三种设施规模的堆肥和厌氧消化系统在不同运输距离下的环境和经济性能，并与当地焚烧进行比较。运输负担通过车辆生命周期评估和电动、燃气、柴油重型卡车的实际数据进行建模。

Result: 结果揭示了规模与运输之间的非线性权衡关系，零距离堆肥的综合影响最低。在所有场景中，45种配置在环境和经济上均优于当地焚烧，111种在环境上更优但成本较高。

Conclusion: 该研究为废物处理基础设施规划提供了量化指导，明确了不同运输距离和处理规模下的最优技术配置选择。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of separate biowaste collection across Europe raises questions regarding environmentally and economically optimal treatment strategies. From this perspective, the balance between decentralised and centralised infrastructure is a potential lever to consider. The choice of technologies is another. Composting and anaerobic digestion (AD) offer viable circular bioeconomy pathways, but their performance depends on transport distance, facility size, seasonal waste fluctuations, and technological efficiency. Threshold distances and scale-dependent interactions affecting the environmental and economic performance of treatment plants remain poorly characterised. This study develops a unified techno-economic and life cycle assessment framework to quantify how transport constraints and treatment capacities shape biowaste valorisation systems. Three facility scales for composting and AD are evaluated across a range of transport distances and compared with local incineration in terms of environmental and economic performance. Transport burdens are modelled using vehicle life cycle assessment (LCA) and realistic heavy-duty truck data for electric-, gas-, and diesel-powered vehicles. Results reveal non-linear trade-offs between scale and transport, with zero-distance composting yielding the lowest combined impacts. Across all scenarios, 45 configurations outperform local incineration economically and environmentally, while 111 remain environmentally preferable despite higher costs, thus providing quantitative guidance for infrastructure planning.

</details>


### [144] [Non-Negative Conjugate Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22121)
*Thomas Schmelzer,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非负共轭梯度法（Non-negative Conjugate Gradients），用于求解带有非负约束和等式约束的二次规划问题。该方法在原始-对偶积极集循环中包装CG，每次迭代在自由变量上求解无约束系统，剔除负变量，重新接纳具有负简化梯度的界变量，并重启CG。该方法在有限次外循环内收敛到唯一全局最小值，且支持无矩阵实现，适用于大规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的共轭梯度法求解对称正定系统$Ax=b$时不保证解的非负性$x\geq0$，而许多实际应用（如图像处理、物理模拟等）要求解满足非负约束。现有方法如投影梯度法收敛较慢（$O(\kappa)$率），而Lawson-Hanson和内点法在处理高维稠密问题时计算成本高。因此需要一种既能保留CG高效收敛特性（$O(\sqrt{\kappa})$率）又能处理非负约束的求解器。

Method: 提出非负共轭梯度法，在原始-对偶积极集循环中包装CG：每步在自由变量上求解无约束系统，剔除变为负的变量，重新接纳具有负简化梯度的界变量，然后重启CG。这些切换等价于线性互补问题$(A,-b)$的主枢轴操作。终止条件采用Júdice和Pires的块主枢轴构造，辅以Bland最小指标回退策略。关键贡献在于将收敛保证扩展到了非精确、无矩阵的Krylov内求解场景：将CG残差与决策边界关联后，近似循环能够做出与精确循环相同的原始和对偶符号决策。

Result: 在合成SPD问题上，循环最多6步外循环即可达到 planted 最优解；使用直接自由集求解时，在高维稠密实例上比Lawson-Hanson和内点法快一个数量级。每个内求解都是无矩阵的，对于$A=M^\top M$，算子$v\mapsto M^\top(Mv)$仅需两次与$M$的乘积，无需$O(n^2)$存储，且保留CG的$O(\sqrt{\kappa})$收敛率。正则化或预条件处理可压缩谱、降低迭代次数，岭回归/Tikhonov分裂还能确保所需的$P$-矩阵性质。

Conclusion: 非负共轭梯度法成功地将CG的快速收敛优势扩展到了带非负约束的问题，在有限次外循环内收敛，且支持无矩阵实现，适用于大规模稀疏或稠密问题。相比投影梯度法（$O(\kappa)$率）和其他专用求解器，该方法在效率上具有显著优势。

Abstract: The conjugate gradient method (CG) solves a symmetric positive definite (SPD) system $Ax=b$, but does not respect $x\geq0$. We develop non-negative conjugate gradients, a solver for the bound-constrained quadratic $\min_x \tfrac12 x^\top A x - b^\top x$ subject to $x\geq0$, $Bx=c$, $A\succ0$, with $Bx=c$ present only in the equality-augmented variant. The solver wraps CG in a primal-dual active-set loop: each step solves the unconstrained system over the free variables, drops variables that turn negative, re-admits any bound variable with negative reduced gradient, and restarts CG. These toggles are the principal pivots of the linear complementarity problem $(A,-b)$, well defined since $A$ is a $P$-matrix. Termination uses the block-principal-pivoting construction of Júdice and Pires, with a least-index Bland fallback, reaching the unique global minimiser in finitely many outer steps. Our contribution carries this guarantee to the inexact, matrix-free regime the Krylov inner solve requires: once CG's residual is tied to the decision margin, the approximate loop makes the same primal and dual sign decisions as the exact one, and a direct factorisation may replace CG unchanged. Each inner solve is matrix-free: for $A=M^\top M$, the operator $v\mapsto M^\top(Mv)$ needs two products with $M$, no $O(n^2)$ storage, and retains CG's $O(\sqrtκ)$ rate, $κ=κ(A)$. Regularisation or preconditioning that compresses the spectrum lowers the iteration count; a ridge/Tikhonov split $A\mapsto(1-α)A+αR^\top R$ also secures the needed $P$-matrix property. A loop-free projected-gradient reference method, analysed alongside, converges at the slower $O(κ)$ rate. On synthetic SPD problems the loop reaches the planted optimum in at most 6 outer steps, and with a direct free-set solve outpaces Lawson--Hanson and an interior-point solver by an order of magnitude on dense instances.

</details>


### [145] [Finite-Time Stabilization of Linear Systems via Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22191)
*Weihao Weng,Yacine Chitour,Paolo Mason*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个最优控制框架，通过引入适当的积分代价函数，推导出一类非线性控制器，实现线性系统的有限时间镇定，并通过HJB方程分析其正则性，数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性系统镇定方法多为渐近稳定，收敛时间无限；需要实现有限时间收敛，同时保证控制的最优性。

Method: 构建合适的积分代价函数，基于最优性原理推导非线性控制器；导出HJB方程并分析其正则性；通过数值仿真验证方法有效性。

Result: 成功推导出一类保证有限时间稳定的非线性最优控制律；HJB方程的正则性得以刻画；数值仿真验证了理论结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的最优控制框架能有效实现线性系统的有限时间镇定，且收敛时间可估计，具有理论完备性和实际可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents an optimal control framework for achieving finite-time stabilization of linear systems. By introducing a suitably constructed integral cost function, we derive a new class of nonlinear controllers that guarantee finite-time stability through the application of the optimality principle. The relationship between the resulting optimal control law and the associated value function is analyzed, leading to the derivation of a Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation and the study of its regularity properties. Numerical simulations validate the theoretical findings and illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Furthermore, a discussion on estimating the convergence time is provided.

</details>


### [146] [Explicit Iteration Complexity of Exact Data-Driven Inverse Optimization for Integer Linear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22263)
*Akira Kitaoka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对数据驱动逆优化问题（DDIOP），特别是整数线性规划（ILP）场景，给出了投影次梯度下降法应用于次优性损失时，实现精确一致性所需迭代次数的显式上界，该上界表示为样本数、特征维度、特征范围及约束系数矩阵结构的显式函数（含多项式因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对于ILP的逆优化问题，虽已知梯度方法可在有限次内精确求解，且迭代次数上界为$T=O(1/γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})^2)$，但问题依赖的几何常数$γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})$无法用问题规模显式下界估计，导致无法给出迭代次数关于问题规模的显式函数。

Method: 将投影次梯度下降法应用于子最优性损失（suboptimality loss），分析ILP前向问题的结构特性，推导出迭代次数关于样本数、特征维度、特征范围、约束系数矩阵结构等参数的显式上界（含多项式因子）。

Result: 给出了投影次梯度下降法在ILP逆优化中达到精确数据一致性所需的迭代次数，作为样本数、特征维度、特征范围、约束系数矩阵结构的完全显式函数。

Conclusion: 为ILP逆优化问题的计算复杂度提供了显式、可量化的理论保证，填补了此前无法用问题规模显式刻画迭代次数下界的空白。

Abstract: A data-driven inverse optimization problem (DDIOP) is the problem of estimating the objective-function parameters (weights) that explain observed optimal-solution data, and it arises in many applications, including integer linear programming (ILP). It is known that, by applying gradient-based optimization methods to the suboptimality loss, the inverse optimization of ILPs can be solved exactly within finitely many oracle iterations, and that the required number of iterations is bounded as $T=O(1/γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})^2)$ in terms of a problem-dependent geometric constant $γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})$. However, no means of bounding $γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})$ from below as a function of the problem size has been available, and hence the number of iterations could not be given as an explicit function of the problem size. We therefore give, when the forward problem is an integer linear program (ILP), the number of iterations sufficient for projected subgradient descent applied to the suboptimality loss to achieve exact consistency with the observed data, as a fully explicit function of the number of samples, the dimension of the features, the ranges of the features, and the structure of the constraint coefficient matrix, up to polynomial factors in the basic constants (the diameter of the weight set, the step-size parameter, and the Lipschitz constant of the suboptimality loss).

</details>


### [147] [Homogeneous Self-Dual Embedding via Perspective Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22278)
*Goran Banjac*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个广义的齐次自对偶嵌入模型，适用于最小化两个正常下半连续凸函数之和的问题，并通过Douglas-Rachford算法求解该嵌入，可同时获得原问题的原始-对偶解或不可行性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的齐次自对偶嵌入模型广泛应用于锥优化，但缺乏针对更一般凸优化问题（最小化两个凸函数之和）的通用嵌入框架。作者旨在推广该模型，使其适用于更广泛的凸优化问题结构。

Method: 1) 提出广义齐次自对偶嵌入模型，将问题表示为使用函数及其共轭的透视函数的单一不等式；2) 采用Douglas-Rachford算法求解该嵌入；3) 通过利用问题结构实现高效计算。

Result: 该算法恢复了解二次锥规划的现有方法作为特例，并在具有非光滑目标函数和非锥约束的凸优化问题类别上展示了通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 广义齐次自对偶嵌入结合Douglas-Rachford算法为更广泛的凸优化问题（包括非光滑目标和非锥约束）提供了一种统一且有效的求解框架，并能同时获取原始-对偶解或不可行性证明。

Abstract: We present a generalization of the well-known homogeneous self-dual embedding model, which is widely used in conic optimization. The new embedding applies to a problem of minimizing the sum of two proper lower-semicontinuous convex functions and can be represented as a single inequality that uses perspectives of these functions and of their conjugates. A solution to the proposed embedding encodes a primal-dual solution to the original problem when available, or an infeasibility certificate otherwise. We then use the Douglas-Rachford algorithm to find a solution to the embedding and discuss its efficient implementation by exploiting the problem structure. The resulting algorithm recovers an existing method for solving quadratic cone programs as a special case. We demonstrate the generality and effectiveness of the algorithm on a class of convex optimization problems with non-smooth objective function and non-conic constraints.

</details>


### [148] [Langevin for Nonconvex Optimization: Exact, Inexact and Zeroth-Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22353)
*Emanuele Naldi,Marco Rando,Lorenzo Rosasco,Silvia Villa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Langevin方法的非凸优化问题，利用加权Csiszár-Kullback-Pinsker不等式和指数矩估计，从相对熵直接导出目标值误差，避免了中间Wasserstein界，获得了对Log-Sobolev常数更优的依赖。分析了精确梯度ULA、非精确梯度ULA（涵盖随机梯度和零阶估计），首次给出了零阶ULA的非渐近全局非凸优化复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非凸优化中，Langevin方法通常关注采样保证而非优化保证。现有分析依赖于Wasserstein距离，导致对Log-Sobolev常数的依赖次优，而该常数在非凸问题中可能随逆温度和维度指数增长。作者旨在建立更直接的从相对熵到目标值误差的路径，以获得更紧的优化复杂度界。

Method: 1) 利用加权Csiszár-Kullback-Pinsker不等式和指数矩估计，建立相对熵与目标值误差之间的直接联系；2) 分析精确梯度的Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (ULA)，给出显式非渐近界；3) 扩展到非精确梯度ULA，允许偏置和随机梯度代理（均方误差最多随状态二次增长）；4) 将高斯和球面有限差分估计纳入该框架，推导零阶Langevin优化的函数评估复杂度界。

Result: 给出了精确梯度ULA在非凸平滑耗散条件下的显式非渐近期望过剩风险界，依赖于逆温度、维度、步长、平滑性和耗散参数以及Log-Sobolev常数。将结果扩展到非精确梯度场景，覆盖了随机梯度和零阶估计。首次建立了零阶ULA的全局非凸优化复杂度非渐近界。

Conclusion: 通过直接相对熵-目标值误差路径，改进了对Log-Sobolev常数的依赖，避免了Wasserstein界。零阶ULA的复杂度界是首个此类结果，数值实验验证了所提方案的有效性。

Abstract: We study Langevin-based methods for non-convex optimization under smoothness and dissipativity assumptions. Our focus is on obtaining non-asymptotic bounds for the expected excess risk rather than sampling guarantees for the full target distribution. The key ingredient of our analysis is a direct passage from relative entropy to objective-value error, based on a weighted Csiszár--Kullback--Pinsker inequality and exponential-moment estimates. This avoids intermediate Wasserstein bounds and yields sharper dependence on the Log-Sobolev constant, a quantity that may scale exponentially with the inverse temperature and the dimension in non-convex problems. We first analyze the Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm with exact gradients and derive explicit bounds on $\mathbb{E}[F(x_k)]-\min F$ in terms of the inverse temperature, dimension, stepsize, smoothness and dissipativity parameters, and the Log-Sobolev constant. We then extend the result to an inexact-gradient version of ULA, allowing for biased and stochastic gradient surrogates whose mean-square error grows at most quadratically in the state. This framework covers stochastic gradients and zeroth-order estimators based only on function evaluations. In particular, we show that both Gaussian and spherical finite-difference estimators fit into the inexact-ULA theory and obtain explicit function-evaluation complexity bounds for zeroth-order Langevin optimization. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first non-asymptotic global non-convex optimization complexity bounds for zeroth-order ULA. We also provide numerical experiments illustrating the behavior of the proposed zeroth-order Langevin schemes.

</details>


### [149] [Data Driven Modeling of Nonlinear Dynamics in a Rotating Detonation Combustor via Finite Dimensional Approximations of the Koopman Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22457)
*David Oexle,Tobias Breiten,Myles D. Bohon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用动态模式分解（DMD）及其变体（结合时间延迟嵌入）对旋转爆震燃烧室（RDC）的高速视频数据构建Koopman算子的有限维近似，成功重构了驻波模式并捕获了非线性波相互作用，同时提出了降低传感器噪声影响的方法，从而为RDC不同运行模式的动力学分析提供了见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转爆震燃烧室（RDC）具有提高推进和发电效率的潜力，但其动力学由连续传播的爆震波主导，存在多种运行模式（包括反向旋转波的非线性相互作用和驻波模式），需要有效的全局线性化分析方法来深入理解其复杂动力学行为。

Method: 利用Koopman算子理论，通过动态模式分解（DMD）及其变体，对柏林工业大学RDC的高速视频数据（自然火焰发光度）构建有限维近似；引入时间延迟嵌入作为观测字典以克服标准DMD的局限性，并采用技术降低传感器噪声影响。

Result: 基于时间延迟嵌入的DMD方法能够准确重构驻波模式并捕获非线性波相互作用；所提出的去噪技术有效减轻了传感器噪声的影响；DMD模型通过将重构信号分解为特征成分，揭示了不同运行模式的动力学特性。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子的DMD方法（特别是结合时间延迟嵌入）是分析RDC复杂非线性动力学的有效工具，能够为不同运行模式提供物理洞察，并具备处理实测噪声数据的能力。

Abstract: A Rotating Detonation Combustor (RDC) is a promising technology for increasing efficiency in propulsion and power generation applications. The dynamics of the RDC are governed by continuously propagating detonation waves within an annular combustion chamber. Multiple operating modes can be observed, including nonlinear interactions between counter-rotating waves and the emergence of standing wave patterns. Koopman operator theory provides a framework to globally linearize nonlinear dynamical systems by representing their evolution in the space of observables rather than states. In this work, finite-dimensional approximations of the Koopman operator are constructed using variants of Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD) applied to high-speed video data capturing the natural flame luminosity of the detonation waves in the RDC at the Technical University (TU) Berlin. By introducing time-delay embeddings as a dictionary of observables, this approach overcomes the limitations of standard DMD methods, particularly for accurate reconstruction of standing wave patterns and for capturing nonlinear interactions. In addition, a technique is presented to mitigate the influence of sensor noise in the luminosity measurements. Finally, it is shown that the DMD-based models provide insight into the dynamics of different operating modes by decomposing the reconstructed signal into its characteristic features.

</details>


### [150] [Fully Convergent Projection-based Methods with Momentum under Nonconvex Geometric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22510)
*Matteo Lapucci,Diego Scuppa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在投影梯度法中集成动量项以加速非线性优化问题的收敛，同时保持收敛到强平稳性条件的理论保证。通过设计预投影步骤的回溯机制，在无需平滑假设下确保收敛到Mordukhovich平稳点，并在特定条件下收敛到Bouligand和Proximally平稳点。数值实验表明该方法在稀疏和低秩约束问题上计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影梯度法已被证明能在弱正则假设下收敛到强平稳性条件，但其收敛速度可以通过引入动量项（非线性优化中常用的加速技术）来提升。然而，直接将动量项替换负梯度会引发内在问题，因此需要设计合适的机制来集成动量项而不损害收敛保证。

Method: 首先指出在投影方法中直接用一般下降方向替代负梯度存在的内在问题；然后提出预投影步骤的回溯机制，允许在方向中集成动量项。该技术无需平滑假设即可确保收敛到Mordukhovich平稳性，前提是基础方向在小步长时渐近回归负梯度；若基础搜索方向在最小步长时完全回归负梯度，则算法可收敛到Bouligand和Proximally平稳点。

Result: 数值实验在稀疏约束和低秩约束两类问题上进行，结果表明所提方法计算有效，能够利用动量项提供的额外信息提升性能。

Conclusion: 动量项可以成功集成到投影梯度法框架中而不破坏收敛保证，通过适当的回溯机制，算法能在不同平滑假设下收敛到多种强平稳性条件，并在实际问题上展现计算优势。

Abstract: Nonlinear optimization problems with complicated, nonconvex, yet geometrically structured constraints can be tackled by projected-gradient methods: under weak regularity assumptions, these approaches were recently proved to possess convergence properties to the strongest stationarity conditions. In this work, we show how momentum terms, commonly used in nonlinear optimization to speed up the convergence process, can be integrated within this algorithmic framework without harming convergence guarantees. Preliminarily, we highlight an intrinsic issue induced by the direct replacement of the negative gradient with a general descent direction within the projected approach. Then, we present suitable backtracking mechanisms for the pre-projection step, allowing us to integrate momentum terms in the direction. By this technique, we can specifically ensure, without any smoothness assumptions, convergence to Mordukhovich stationarity as long as the base directions asymptotically revert to the negative gradient for small stepsizes; moreover, if the base search direction reverts exactly to the negative gradient for the smallest steps, the algorithm is proved to converge to Bouligand and Proximally stationary points, with and without (local) smoothness assumptions respectively. Finally, the proposed procedure is numerically tested on some classes of problems, namely, sparsity and bounded-rank constrained problems; the results indicate that the proposed method is computationally effective, taking advantage of the additional information provided by the momentum term.

</details>
